Version 15.11.0 → 15.12.0 in both manifests (descriptions updated IN PLACE per the anti-rebloat rule). **Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 41 skills / 22 hooks (scripts are uncounted); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.11.0.

**v15.11.0 — sdk-spike token levers: per-role effort + opt-in per-subtask task budget + first-class token accounting (quarantine unchanged, additive):** Executes token-economy item 03 entirely inside the quarantined `loomwright/sdk-spike/` runner (still UNCOUNTED, referenced only by the opt-in `--sdk-runner` seam; default path byte-identical with the flag off). **(1) Runner config layer:** a `ROLE_CONFIG` table encodes per-role effort defaults (worker `medium`, reviewer `high`) resolved via `resolveRoleConfig()` with precedence `--worker-effort`/`--reviewer-effort` > `--effort` (both roles) > table; invalid effort or budget values fail CLOSED (non-zero exit, no query issued). Opt-in `--task-budget N` applies per WORKER query only (reviewers deliberately get no `taskBudget`): the documented 20k minimum is enforced runner-side (< 20000 fails closed — the SDK type has no floor), and when unset the field is omitted entirely from `Options`. **(2) Per-subtask token accounting:** every subtask entry in the EXECUTE_RESULT-equivalent output carries an additive `token_usage` object aggregated from its worker + reviewer `query()` results — real `usage`/`total_cost_usd`/`num_turns` in live mode, including `QueryFailedError` fold-back of a failing query's real spend into the failed entry; `proxy: true`-labeled zeros on `--dry-run` (token counts never invented; fixtures untouched — usage is message-level, not payload-level). **(3) Context editing / history pruning: NOT exposed by the pinned SDK 0.3.202** — recorded as a capability-matrix gap (only PreCompact/PostCompact hooks + `getContextUsage()`); the pin is unchanged. **(4) Self-test 25/25 → 41/41** — new offline checks for config parsing (valid/invalid effort), the budget floor, budget omission, accounting fields in dry-run output, and positive-path effort-precedence + worker-only taskBudget passthrough assertions (via an opt-in `SDK_SPIKE_TRACE_OPTS=1` dry-run stderr trace of the resolved per-role opts; default dry-run output byte-stable), and a `throw-usage-worker` fixture set proving the worker arm of the QueryFailedError fold-back offline (reviewer arm was already covered); all pass-count citations updated. **(5) Docs:** `docs/SPIKES/SDK_RUNNER_SPIKE.md` capability matrix gains 3 doc-verified rows (effort SUPPORTED `sdk.d.ts:1620`/`:522`; taskBudget SUPPORTED @alpha `sdk.d.ts:1647-1649`, beta header `task-budgets-2026-03-13`; context editing NOT-EXPOSED-BY-SDK) + a token-accounting note; live verification of the taskBudget/effort/QueryFailedError paths is PENDING (first live eval run). `docs/SPIKES/FABLE_PARITY_EVAL.md` gains an additive datestamped amendment (amended 2026-07-18, before any run) adding token-cost-per-subtask as a RECORDED secondary observable — explicitly NOT a decision input; the 1.5× decision rule is byte-unchanged and the Results table was verified still empty. Version 15.10.0 → 15.11.0 in both manifests (descriptions updated IN PLACE per the anti-rebloat rule). **Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 41 skills / 22 hooks (sdk-spike stays quarantined and uncounted); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.10.0.

**v15.10.0 — `check-token-budget.sh`: CI-enforced per-agent prompt-inventory budgets (additive):** Executes token-economy item 02 — a prompt-weight ratchet in the doc-currency-gate mold. **(1) NEW `scripts/check-token-budget.sh`** (repo-root CI validator): for each `loomwright/agents/*.md`, computes the OFFLINE PROXY weight (`bytes / 4`) of the agent prompt PLUS every skill preloaded via its frontmatter `skills:` list (frontmatter-bounded parsing — body bullets never leak in), compares against a declared per-agent budget, prints a per-agent proxy report, and exits 1 on any breach, missing-preloaded-SKILL.md reference, or undeclared budget. Deterministic, offline, bash-3.2 safe (`wc -c` sizing, no `${var//…}`, no GNU-only stat/sed/date). It is a **PROXY on prompt inventory growth, NOT live tokenizer inflation** — never calls `count_tokens`, and every number is labeled "proxy tokens." **(2) NEW `loomwright/docs/prompt-token-budgets.json`** — the single authoritative budget source the gate reads (keyed by agent filename stem; `proxy_bytes_per_token`, per-agent `budget`/`measured`/`note`, and the documented raise rule: raise the budget in the SAME PR that breaches it, with a one-line `note`, visible in the diff). Initial budgets = measured proxy value + ~10% headroom, so no agent fails at introduction (a ratchet, not a diet). **(3) `loomwright/docs/ARCHITECTURE_CONTRACTS.md` §"Prompt Token Budgets"** — mirrors the JSON for humans (14-row table + proxy caveat + raise rule). **(4) NEW `scripts/test-check-token-budget.sh`** — hermetic offline self-test (pass / breach / missing-preloaded-skill / no-budget / frontmatter-bounded-parsing / empty-agents-dir / inline-flow-style-skills / orphaned-budget / live-repo cases) via the gate's `TOKEN_BUDGET_*` env overrides. The gate also fails CLOSED on an unsupported inline/flow-style `skills:` list and on an orphaned budget entry (a budget key with no matching agent `.md`). **(5) Wired into `.github/workflows/ci.yml`** as its own self-test-then-gate step (repo-root `scripts/test-*.sh` is NOT covered by the plugin-internal `loomwright/scripts/test-*.sh` loop, so it is wired explicitly). Version 15.9.0 → 15.10.0 in both manifests (descriptions updated IN PLACE per the anti-rebloat rule). **Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 41 skills / 22 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.9.0.

**v15.9.0 — token ledger + insights Token economics + spawn-contract prompt-cache discipline (additive):** Ships the token-ledger / cache-audit job as three additive layers plus this version bump. **(1) Fail-SAFE `token_ledger` SubagentStop emitter (`scripts/emit-token-ledger.sh`):** chained onto the existing `send-telemetry.sh` hook command lines (stdin fan-out — **hook count stays 22**; no new matcher entries). Prefers real usage fields when present; otherwise appends a transcript-byte proxy (`proxy:true`, `token_proxy_kind:"transcript_bytes"`) — never invents token counts, never labelled as tokens. **Session-id join:** prefers the active plugin session id from `.supervisor/state.md` (`status: running|checkpoint`) so ledger lines land in the same `.supervisor/logs/{session_id}.jsonl` as `session_end` (job 04 / per-run attribution); retains the Claude Code UUID as additive `cc_session_id`; falls back to the UUID when no active run is present. Covered by `scripts/test-token-ledger.sh` + fixtures; authority in `docs/TELEMETRY.md` §Token ledger. **(2) `/insights` advisory `## Token economics`:** `build-insights.sh` aggregates `token_ledger` JSONL (real usage vs proxy) after Cost / before Recent sessions; degrades when absent; dollar cost remains ccusage. Extended `test-insights.sh`. **(3) Spawn-contract stable-prefix reorder + AGENT_GUIDELINES rule:** `skills/async-orchestration/SKILL.md` Part 2 Subagent Spawn Contracts reordered so stable prompt content precedes volatile interpolations (prompt caching is prefix-match); skill → 1.2.0 + SKILLS_INDEX sync; `AGENT_GUIDELINES.md` gains the durable **Stable-prefix-first spawn prompts** rule. Version 15.8.0 → 15.9.0 in both manifests (descriptions updated IN PLACE per the anti-rebloat rule). **Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 41 skills / 22 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.8.0.

**v15.8.0 — feat(spike): Fable-parity — quarantined Agent SDK runner spike + opt-in `--sdk-runner` seam + opt-in `--multi-voter-heal` Phase 4.5 verification + pre-registered FABLE_PARITY_EVAL protocol:** Executes the 5-subtask fable-parity job (review-remediation item 09; roadmap item 11 DEFERRED → SPIKED), closing the prompt-vs-code orchestration gap in the smallest falsifiable way. **(1) Quarantined SDK spike runner (`loomwright/sdk-spike/`, UNCOUNTED):** a TypeScript port of ONLY Execute Manager's Phase 3 poll loop using `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk` (pinned `^0.3.202`, committed lockfile — TS chosen because it bundles the CC binary): tolerant brief parsing (`## Subtask Structure` + `### Subtask contracts`), `requires`-driven wave scheduling replacing the prompt poll loop with in-code `Promise.all`, one `query()` per worker/reviewer with `output_format: json_schema` derived from WORKER_RESULT v2 / CODE_REVIEW_RESULT v3 field shapes (schema_versions preserved; the v12 outputs gate mirrored), external git-worktree isolation with commit-before-remove on deterministic `sdk-spike/subtask-N` branches (stale branch = fail closed), fail-closed structured-output handling (`error_max_structured_output_retries` / missing payload / failed local re-validation ⇒ `subtasks_failed`, nothing fabricated), and an EXECUTE_RESULT-equivalent output block — plus a fully offline `--dry-run` mode exercised by `test/self-test.sh` (25/25, degrades gracefully without node_modules). The directory is quarantined: not an agent/command/skill/hook, referenced ONLY by the opt-in `--sdk-runner` seam (default OFF; `dist/` is gitignored so nothing resolves until a one-time build) — no default/always-on path reaches it; uncounted and absent from every plugin manifest. **(2) `--sdk-runner` opt-in seam (EXPERIMENTAL, default OFF):** documented in `commands/supervisor.md` + `agents/supervisor.md` Phase 3 + `skills/supervisor-config/SKILL.md` step 2.8 — when passed, Phase 3 shells out to `node "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/sdk-spike/dist/runner.js"` instead of Task-spawning execute-manager; **fail CLOSED** with `error: "sdk_runner_unavailable"` if `node` or the built runner is absent (never a silent fallback; `dist/` is gitignored — build once with `npm install && npm run build`); an explicit FINALIZE delta (verify branches-ahead instead of dirty worktrees, skip in-worktree commit, tolerate removed worktrees + delete runner branches after merge); byte-identical default path with the flag off. **(3) `--multi-voter-heal` opt-in Phase 4.5 verification (default OFF; config `.multi_voter_heal`; `--no-multi-voter-heal` wins):** the review step spawns TWO independent parallel reviewers on the integrated diff — the existing `code-reviewer` (its CODE_REVIEW_RESULT stays the sole gating signal) plus a `red-team-reviewer` verification vote — and a BLOCKING/HIGH `new` finding is fixed ONLY if it survives the other lens's refute check (at most ONE refute spawn per lens per iteration; refuted findings LOGGED, not fixed); per-run `findings_raised`/`findings_refuted`/`findings_fixed` counters carried in `SUPERVISOR_RESULT.summary` (additive prose — schema_version stays 1, noted in `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md`); heal_decision semantics, `--heal-iterations` bounds, never-merge, and the completion tail unchanged; authority (incl. the standalone `--red-team` lens interaction — independent flags, no aliasing) single-sourced in `skills/self-heal-advisory/SKILL.md` Part 2 §Multi-voter verification. **(4) Per-stage model/effort routing (Part C):** `docs/ARCHITECTURE_CONTRACTS.md` §Cost Profiles gains a `phase45-multi-voter-verification` row (default inherit; `--cheap` ⇒ sonnet for both voter spawns + the refute spawn) + a strongest-tier-when-not-cheap note; recommended-routing comments added to code-reviewer + red-team-reviewer frontmatter (comment-only, `model: inherit` unchanged); the `--cheap` role enumerations in CLAUDE.md / agent-help / supervisor / autonomous docs extended with the multi-voter voters. **(5) Spike record + pre-registered eval (this entry):** NEW `docs/SPIKES/SDK_RUNNER_SPIKE.md` — the doc-verified 7-row SDK capability matrix, parity matrix (ported cleanly / SDK-can't-or-differs incl. hooks.json-firing + skills-preload/memory NEEDS VERIFICATION / residual divergences with their FINALIZE backstop), TS choice, dry-run timing (~0.03s wall, 0 tokens for the full 2-subtask control flow) with the live comparison PENDING + exact instructions, and a **provisional GO/NO-GO** (GO for a v16 runner ONLY if the eval's arm 3 wins, else cut); NEW `docs/SPIKES/FABLE_PARITY_EVAL.md` — pre-registered 5-requirements × 3-arms protocol (bare Claude Code / Loomwright default / Loomwright + both opt-ins; metrics: review rounds to READY, heal_iterations, independent post-merge `/code-reviewer` defect findings, wall tokens; SDK runner graduates ONLY if arm 3 beats arm 2 on defects or rounds without >1.5× token cost; Loomwright must beat arm 1 or the losing layers get cut; scratch branches only, NO PRs to main, 15-run hard cap, empty results table, no metric added after the first run; the Part-B ≥1 refuted-finding-not-fixed demonstration folded into arm-3 runs); `docs/IMPROVEMENTS_ROADMAP.md` item 11 verdict swept in all three places; version 15.7.0 → 15.8.0 in both manifests (descriptions updated IN PLACE per the anti-rebloat rule). **Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 41 skills / 22 hooks (sdk-spike is quarantined and uncounted); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.7.0 — both new flags are opt-in, default OFF; every default path is byte-identical.

**v15.7.0 — knowledge-corpus curation: human-gated churn-ledger retract/supersede + staleness, lessons `retract` tombstones, `/insights` corpus health, pre-registered advisory-loop eval scaffold:** Executes the 4-subtask "Knowledge-corpus curation" job — the curation half of the Twin: the knowledge corpora (churn ledger + lessons store) can now forget, human-gated and auditable, with the loop's value to be measured (not assumed) via a pre-registered counterfactual eval. **(1) NEW `loomwright/scripts/curate-postmortem.sh`** — the human-gated **sole curator writer** for the churn ledger: `curate-postmortem.sh retract|supersede --target <key> --reason <text> [--replacement <pr_url>] --confirm` appends exactly ONE jq-built curation record `{"schema_version":1,"source":"curation","curation_action":"retract"|"supersede","target_key":…,"replacement":…,"reason":…,"ts":…}` to `.supervisor/postmortem/results.jsonl` (`target_key` matches a data line's `automate_key` OR `pr_url` by EXACT string equality; `--replacement` rejected on retract; newline/CR targets rejected). WITHOUT `--confirm` it prints the exact would-append JSON line and exits 1 (dry-run — this writer NEVER writes unattended); append-only (never rewrites a ledger line), read-back-verified, jq-only construction, fail-LOUD exit 2/3 (bimodal failure philosophy: writers/gates fail loud, readers fail safe). ADDITIVE — POSTMORTEM_RESULT stays `schema_version: 1`; old readers fail-safe-skip curation records via the `changed_paths` overlap filter. **(2) `read-postmortem.sh` curation + staleness:** the advisory reader now EXCLUDES any data entry whose `automate_key` or `pr_url` is named by a well-formed curation record (presence-disciplined via jq `has()` — a record whose `target_key` is missing/null/non-string/empty curates NOTHING; targeted entries stay live) and never counts a curation record as a churn hit; it also drops data entries whose parseable `ts` is older than `CHURN_STALE_DAYS` (env override, default 180 days; non-numeric → 180; missing/unparseable `ts` = FRESH, fail-open via jq `fromdateiso8601?`). EMPTY⇒silent and always-exit-0 preserved. **(3) `write-lessons.sh` `retract` verb:** `write-lessons.sh retract <category> <lesson-text>` (or `retract --hash <64-hex>`) tombstones a lesson — verifies the target is present in LESSONS.md AND chain-trusted (last-action-wins walk), exits 4 on an absent/untrusted target (never tombstones silently), then appends a chain-valid `action:"retract"` provenance entry (the auditable tombstone — provenance is never deleted) and atomically rewrites LESSONS.md with only the entry line removed (the `## <category>` heading stays). `read-lessons.sh` mirrors last-action-wins (a chain-valid retract untrusts; a later re-add re-trusts) and logs tombstoned lines with a distinct `RETRACTED` label (vs `DROPPED` for out-of-band poison). **(4) `/insights` `## Corpus health`:** new always-rendered advisory dashboard section in `build-insights.sh` — churn ledger entries/curated/stale (>`CHURN_STALE_DAYS`) + lessons entries/retracted/stale (>`LESSON_STALE_DAYS`, default 90, matching `read-lessons.sh`) — keyed ONLY off the record shapes (jq-counted, per-line tolerant), degrades gracefully (absent corpora → a note; never gates, never exits non-zero). **(5) NEW `loomwright/docs/SPIKES/ADVISORY_LOOP_EVAL.md`** — the pre-registered counterfactual-eval protocol for the advisory seams (north-star "earn surface area with evidence"): metrics (review rounds to READY via POSTMORTEM_RESULT `review_rounds` (= the drain's `REVIEW_HEAL_RESULT` `fix_cycles`) / `/pr-postmortem`, Phase 4.5 `heal_iterations`, `rubric_score` where present) + the decision rule (no measurable benefit ⇒ cut the losing seams; measurable benefit ⇒ the number goes in the marketplace description at the next release) declared BEFORE any run; protocol = 6–10 historical requirements from `.supervisor/jobs/done/`, 3–5 paired scratch-branch re-runs via in-session `/autonomous` with seams disabled vs enabled (OAuth-token constraint — no API-based harness; NO PRs to main; token cost capped at 5 re-runs); the seam-disable switch design (temporary `--no-advisory` master switch vs per-seam env gates — default-ON-advisory, opt-out only) deferred to the eval-execution brief. Status: pre-registered — paired runs pending (post-merge). **(6) Tests + docs sweep (this entry):** NEW `loomwright/scripts/test-curate-postmortem.sh` suite; `test-lessons.sh` + `test-insights.sh` extended; `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md` gains the `source: "curation"` record-variant subsection + a Version History entry; version 15.6.0 → 15.7.0 in both manifests (descriptions updated IN PLACE per the anti-rebloat rule). **Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 41 skills / 22 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.6.0 — with one documented advisory-output change: the churn reader now drops entries older than `CHURN_STALE_DAYS` (default 180d, env-overridable), so aged pre-existing ledgers surface fewer prior-churn hits than before (record schema itself strictly additive).

**v15.6.0 — stackpack + mysql-mcp spin-off: 18 tech-stack skills and the bundled MySQL MCP server move to sibling plugins (marketplace now lists 3 plugins):** Executes the tech-stack-skills spin-off (review-remediation item 07, user-expanded to include the MCP extraction). Sharpens Loomwright's identity as the orchestration core — the dormant, stack-specific knowledge library identified in `docs/SPIKES/NORTH_STAR_DIRECTION.md`'s bloat finding is extracted, not deleted. **(1) NEW sibling plugin `stackpack` (v1.0.0):** the 18 generic tech-stack skills `git mv`-ed out of `loomwright/skills/` — `nextjs-routing`, `nextjs-components`, `nextjs-api-routes`, `nextjs-data-fetching`, `nextjs-auth`, `nestjs-controllers`, `nestjs-services`, `nestjs-guards`, `nestjs-typeorm`, `nestjs-drizzle`, `gateway-auth-middleware`, `gateway-correlation`, `gateway-proxy-patterns`, `gateway-rate-limiting`, `mysql`, `postgresql`, `redis-caching`, `docker` (dependency audit PASSED: none was agent-preloaded; their cross-links stay intact inside stackpack). Stackpack ships skills only — no agents/commands/hooks. **(2) NEW sibling plugin `mysql-mcp` (v1.0.0):** the read-only MySQL MCP server (`vikashruhil-mysql-mcp` via uvx) moves out of loomwright — `loomwright/.mcp.json` removed and the plugin.json `mcpServers` block removed; the `/setup mysql-mcp` module stays in loomwright as status+guidance pointing at the new plugin. **(3) Loomwright reference sweep:** SKILLS_INDEX pruned to 41 rows + a moved-note; staying-skill Related-skills pointers (error-handling, unit-testing, monitoring-observability, ci-cd, frontend-ui) rewritten to "(stackpack@atelier plugin)" install pointers; agent/command illustrative examples (code-reviewer, orchestrator, launch-pad, plan-reviewer, worker, commands/code-reviewer, commands/orchestrator) genericized to staying skills or `skills/{domain}/SKILL.md` placeholders — zero `skills/<moved-name>/` paths survive in `loomwright/`; NORTH_STAR_DIRECTION.md prune-action stamped SHIPPED; descriptions updated IN PLACE (anti-rebloat) dropping the "bundled read-only MySQL MCP server" clause; counts swept 59 → 41 across every current-claim surface. **Migration note:** if your workflows relied on the moved skills or the MCP server, install the new plugins — `/plugin install stackpack@atelier` and `/plugin install mysql-mcp@atelier`. **Counts: 14 agents / 21 commands / 41 skills (59 → 41: 18 moved to stackpack) / 22 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.5.0.

**v15.5.0 — script-test gap closure: WorktreeRemove hook (roadmap item #6 closed), `|| true` fail-safe sweep, two hardened test suites, advisory LSP for implementation agents:** Executes the 5-subtask "Close script-test gaps" job. **(1) `loomwright/scripts/test-webhook.sh` gains cases 10–19:** hostile-string EXACT round-trip proofs on the gate path (`--context`/`--session-id`) and the `supervisor_result` path (flat top-level fallback + in-block yaml_field line), paused-event format-branch coverage (ntfy.sh URL auto-detect / explicit `LOOMWRIGHT_WEBHOOK_FORMAT=ntfy` / default JSON — which is also the Slack shape, `send-webhook.sh` has no dedicated Slack branch), and exit-0-on-every-failure-path assertions; when the OFF-path is asserted, `LOOMWRIGHT_WEBHOOK_FORMAT` is scrubbed via `env -u` so a dev-environment export can never flip the asserted branch. **(2) NEW `loomwright/scripts/test-notify-desktop.sh` (40 cases):** PATH-sandboxed deterministic self-test for `notify-desktop.sh` in the `test-webhook.sh` harness style — the SUT runs with PATH pointing at a temp bin dir of coreutils symlinks plus stub notifier/timeout binaries that record markers instead of firing real notifications; asserts the fail-safe emitter invariant (RC=0 on EVERY path: missing jq, malformed payload, unknown event, opt-out, scope gate, debounce coalescing, headless Linux); Darwin/Linux dispatch branches are host-guarded (`uname -s`), bash-3.2 + Linux-CI safe. **(3) `hooks.json` (21 → 22 entries):** NEW `WorktreeRemove` `type: command` hook mirroring `WorktreeCreate` — appends `WORKTREE_REMOVED` lines to `.supervisor/logs/worktrees.log`, closing the cleanup-verification half of `docs/IMPROVEMENTS_ROADMAP.md` item #6 (the last OPEN item, now RESOLVED; event verified supported per the official Claude Code hooks docs) — plus a `|| true` belt-and-suspenders sweep on 11 `type: command` hook invocations (3 telemetry, 2 webhook, 2 notify-desktop, session-resume, set-otel-resource-attrs, hook-dispatch-on-pr-create, validate-launch-pad-result) so the fail-SAFE emitter invariant holds at the hook layer even if a script itself exits non-zero. **(4) Advisory LSP wiring (never-gating):** `LSP` added to the `tools:` frontmatter of `worker.md` (Step 5 verify — diagnostics on modified files, advisory input only), `qa-executor.md` (static-discovery diagnostics as a test-targeting signal), and `launch-pad.md` (go-to-definition/references grounding for the file impact map); `docs/ARCHITECTURE_CONTRACTS.md` capability matrix rows updated to `yes (+ LSP)` — closes the CODE_GRAPH_OWNERSHIP spike's last Active Bet-1 item. **(5) Docs/counts sweep (this entry):** version 15.4.0 → 15.5.0 in both manifests (description updated in place per the anti-rebloat rule), hook-count 21 → 22 across all current-claim surfaces, WorktreeRemove rows added to the CLAUDE.md hook table + mirrors, roadmap item #6 flipped OPEN → RESOLVED (0 items OPEN), and a `CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT=` pin added alongside `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID=` in `test-notify-desktop.sh`'s sandboxed runner env blocks (review follow-up). **Counts: 14 agents / 21 commands / 59 skills / 22 hooks (21 → 22: `WorktreeRemove` is NEW); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.4.0.

**v15.4.0 — Supervisor prompt refactor: phase protocol bodies extracted into 4 authority skills (structure-only, ZERO behavior change):** Executes the 5-slice supervisor-prompt-refactor brief (review-remediation item 05). `agents/supervisor.md` shrinks 1652 → 748 lines; every gate, invariant, and hook-validated contract stays in the agent file, and each phase stanza gains an explicit "Protocol authority" Read pointer. **Slice A:** Phase 1.5 PRE-FLIGHT SYNC protocol → NEW `skills/preflight-sync/SKILL.md` (1.0.0; read at Phase 1.5 entry — deliberately NOT preloaded). **Slice B:** the Phase 4.5 SELF_HEAL loop protocol (on-entry actions, base-mismatch cleanup, bounded review-and-fix loop, rubric grading, advisory red-team lens, completion-tail procedure steps 1–6, hard-signal dual emission, error-handling table) → `skills/self-heal-advisory/SKILL.md` Part 2 (→ 1.1.0); the completion-tail guard (step 0) stays VERBATIM in the agent. **Slice C:** Phase 0 INIT (env detection, resume-state loading + the v15.3.0 fail-closed Resume validation gate, config prompts, cost-profile resolution, base-branch + non-interactive preamble, directory bootstrap) → NEW `skills/supervisor-config/SKILL.md` (1.0.0; read at Phase 0 entry — NOT preloaded), plus dedup of the duplicated Output-example block. **Slice D:** Phase 4 FINALIZE merge/PR mechanics + the verbatim Subagent Spawn Contracts + the Git Worktree Lifecycle phase sequence → `skills/async-orchestration/SKILL.md` Part 2 (→ 1.1.1 — the patch also fixes the pre-existing Part 1 contradiction by retitling "Phase 2 (PLAN): Create Branches" to Phase 3 (EXECUTE — Execute Manager Step 2a) ownership, which the file already asserted elsewhere). **Slice E (this sweep):** Session Logging event catalog + `session_end` field spec moved verbatim to `skills/state-management/SKILL.md` §"Session Logging (moved from agents/supervisor.md)" (1.2.0 → 1.3.0; the agent keeps the log-path convention + the emit-`session_end` requirement + the flat-field hard-contract warning); Output Format example compressed to ENVIRONMENT + Phase 1/4.5/5 excerpts (per-phase Output pointers define the other shapes); the duplicated SUPERVISOR_RESULT example, Phase 3 parse pseudo-code (folded into the EXECUTE error table), Input Format flag-echo, and Integration Notes deduplicated; doc-currency sweep (counts 57 → 59 everywhere current-claimed, incl. the SKILLS_INDEX `Total:` line and `/agent-help` tree). Zero behavior change verified by gate/error-string census (`preflight_overlap_detected`, `skip_self_heal_requested`, `phase45_review_invoked`, `heal_decision`, budgets 50/60 all present in the agent file) + the 5 repo validators + plugin self-tests + a six-path dynamic state-trace (fresh / `--continue` / fast-path / parallel / `--skip-self-heal` / `--non-interactive`). `commands/supervisor.md` mirrored per slice — §"Inline-path canonical state writes" survives. SUPERVISOR_RESULT schema untouched. **Counts: 14 agents / 21 commands / 59 skills (57 → 59: `preflight-sync` + `supervisor-config` are NEW) / 21 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.3.0.

**v15.3.0 — fail-closed resume validation gate for `.supervisor/state.md` (`resume_state_invalid`; prompt-surface, additive):** `/supervisor --continue` now refuses to act on a loaded state file whose `## Session` block doesn't parse against the known schema — the same fail-closed philosophy as the Phase 1.5 preflight and rubric gates, closing the one remaining trust-unvalidated-local-state hole (review-remediation item 04, P0: every result block is hook-validated; resume state was not, and a corrupted/stale/hand-edited file could steer the run toward FINALIZE-like behavior). The gate lives ONCE at the earliest consumption point (`agents/supervisor.md` Phase 0 INIT step 2, BEFORE any loaded state — including `cost_profile` hydration — is consumed); the Phase 1 ACQUIRE resume read references that gate instead of duplicating the enums (mirror-drift avoidance). Contract (authoritative in `skills/state-management/SKILL.md` §"Resume validation gate"; skill version 1.1.0 → 1.2.0 + the SKILLS_INDEX.md row in the same change, per the v15.2.3 parity gate): the `## Session` block must exist; `phase` ∈ `INIT | ACQUIRE | PLAN | EXECUTE | FINALIZE | SELF_HEAL | LOOP` and `status` ∈ `running | paused | completed | completed_with_escalation | failed` (verbatim from §"State File Schema"; `PRE_FLIGHT_SYNC` is a `record_decision`-only Decisions Log label, NOT a valid state-file phase); an asserted `branch:` must `git rev-parse --verify`. ANY violation ⇒ refuse the resume with `SUPERVISOR_RESULT.status: failed` + the closed additive error `resume_state_invalid` (documented in `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md` prose per the `preflight_overlap_detected` precedent; NO `schema_version` bump; deliberately NOT an AUTONOMOUS_RUN `status_reason` — the autonomous loop never invokes `--continue`, so `supervisor_failed_other` case (a) is the catch-all) and a user instruction to inspect/delete `.supervisor/state.md` or start fresh — NEVER a silent fresh-start fallback, and NO `--skip-*`/`--force-resume` escape hatch in v1 (deleting the bad state file IS the escape hatch). READ-side gate only: the Context-Keeper sole-writer contract and inline-path write responsibility are untouched. Dynamic trace across the five resume cases (valid file / missing file / unknown phase / unknown status / valid-but-branch-gone) confirms the happy path and the missing-file fresh start are unchanged — only invalid files see new behavior. Mirrored in `commands/supervisor.md` (`--continue` Parameters-row note + a Troubleshooting entry) and a CLAUDE.md Common-Pitfalls line. Prompt-surface only — no script/hook change. **Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 57 skills / 21 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.2.3.

**v15.2.3 — SKILLS_INDEX version-cell CI parity gate + IMPROVEMENTS_ROADMAP de-staling (doc-hygiene):** Kills two documented recurring drift sources (review-remediation item 03, P0). **(1) New repo-root validator `scripts/check-skills-index-sync.sh`** (bash-3.2-safe, no network, pure grep/awk in the `check-doc-currency.sh` style): for every `loomwright/skills/*/SKILL.md` carrying a `version:` frontmatter field, it asserts `SKILLS_INDEX.md` has exactly ONE table row — keyed structurally on the backticked dir-path cell (`` `supervisor-readiness/` ``), never the display name, so naming mismatches cannot false-positive — whose Version cell equals the frontmatter value (failing with a per-skill diff list), and additionally asserts no index row references a nonexistent skill dir (ghost rows), no duplicate rows, and no malformed version cells. Structural only: it reads the first frontmatter block and table rows, never dated changelog prose. Wired into `.github/workflows/ci.yml` as its own hard-gate step next to the other 4 repo-root validators (it is a repo-root script, deliberately NOT the plugin-internal `test-*.sh` nullglob loop), running `--self-test` first: a fully synthetic mktemp fixture (2 fake skills + index) proving aligned→pass plus fail-loud negative cases covering every DRIFT branch (the authoritative case list lives in the script's self_test) — each asserted to exit non-zero and name the offending skill. **(2) The one live drifted row fixed:** supervisor-readiness 1.1.1 → 1.1.2 (all other 56 rows re-verified aligned at execution time; index follows skill — no SKILL.md `version`/`lastUpdated` touched). **(3) `docs/IMPROVEMENTS_ROADMAP.md` de-staled:** a dated top banner marks it a pre-hooks.json-centralization planning snapshot (current-state authority: CLAUDE.md + hooks.json + plugin.json) with a verdict summary covering ALL 18 items, and each `### N.` item header gains an inline re-verified `**[VERDICT: RESOLVED|DEFERRED|OPEN — evidence]**` line — **10 RESOLVED (1, 2, 4, 7, 8, 13, 14, 15, 16, 17) / 7 DEFERRED (3, 5, 9, 10, 11, 12, 18) / 1 OPEN (6 — `WorktreeCreate` shipped, `WorktreeRemove` hook still absent from `hooks.json`)**. Re-verification against the live repo flipped items 4 (`red-team-reviewer.md` has `effort: xhigh`), 13 (RED_TEAM_RESULT + LAUNCH_PAD_RESULT schemas both exist in RESULT_SCHEMAS.md), and 15 (no "Supervisor v3"/"v5.x"/"v7.x" refs remain in AGENT_GUIDELINES.md) to RESOLVED versus the 2026-07-05 review's OPEN hypothesis, and item 8 to RESOLVED-in-substance (`loomwright/scripts/` ships 80+ runtime helpers). Items 12 and 14 (omitted by the requirement's enumeration) were classified from evidence (DEFERRED / RESOLVED); the requirement's "19 items" vs the document's actual 18 is a count discrepancy, not a missing item. Edits additive only — no item bodies deleted. **(4) `/setup` Quick Start pointer:** one line in both `README.md` and `.claude-plugin/README.md` Quick Starts. A repo-root script is uncounted — Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 57 skills / 21 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.

**v15.2.2 — direct deterministic self-test for `send-telemetry-core.sh` (tests-only):** New `loomwright/scripts/test-send-telemetry-core.sh` (auto-registered by the CI `test-*.sh` glob; review-remediation item 02, P0) unit-tests the telemetry core's privacy/consent/dedup pipeline directly — the one component whose regression could leak user data to a public GitHub issue. The existing `test-telemetry.sh` covers the wrapper-level fixture×consent matrix; this harness pins the core itself with a 7-group matrix (83 assertions): **(1) privacy true-positives** — one runtime-generated payload per `PRIVACY_PATTERNS` label ×9 (openai-key, github-token, api-key, bearer, password, macos-home-path, linux-home-path, email, env-assignment), each constructed to match no earlier pattern in list order and pinned to the REAL exit 2 plus the exact `PRIVACY_BLOCKED pattern=<label>` stderr line; **(2) true-negatives** — near-misses (`sk-short`, `ghp_short`, `apikey` in prose without `=`, a bare `/Users/` segment) pass the scan and stop at consent (3, never 2); **(3) ordering guarantee** — the v11.2.0 raw-scan-before-consent regression pin: a healthy (score≥5, success) payload carrying a secret exits 2, never 5, even with consent+repo fully configured — plus a clean-twin counterfactual proving the same shape without the secret IS interest-skipped (5); **(4) consent matrix** — absent → 3 `state=missing`; `{"telemetry":"no"}` → 3 with the distinct `denied — skipped` marker; malformed JSON → **fail-closed** 3 `state=parse_error` (asserted from the actual code path — no bug found); `always_allow` without repo → 4; `always_allow` + malformed repo (non-empty, no slash) → 1 with the `invalid_repo_format` marker (even under `--dry-run` — no dry-run short-circuit before the format gate); `always_allow`+repo+`--dry-run` → would-send `WOULD_EXIT=0`; env-var repo precedence over the consent file; **(5) nullable/missing-key discipline** (PR #84 lesson) — missing key, explicit `null`, and non-string values for BOTH consent fields all fail closed; **(6) dedup determinism** — `telemetry-sent.log` is appended only on the live path, so the sandbox log is seeded: same `task_id::bucket::primary_error` sha256 within the 6h window → 5 `dedup_hit`; different `primary_error` and a stale (>6h) same-hash entry → not deduped; **(7) redaction visibility** — `[REDACTED:<label>]` markers ×9 exercised against the **extracted production STAGE1_PY source** (a secret-bearing payload exits 2 before its body ever prints, so the harness `exec()`s the real stage-1 code verbatim and calls its `redact_text`/`scan_for_secret` — never a duplicated regex copy; also pins the 9-label list order). `gh` is proven never-invoked by a fail-loud PATH shim (exit 97 + marker file, asserted absent at the end) with live invocations only on short-circuit paths (2/3/4/5) and dry-run `WOULD_EXIT` assertions on would-send paths; all state lives under a mktemp sandbox (the core's consent/log paths are `$PWD`-based per `send-telemetry-core.sh:42-44`) and the real `.supervisor/` is checksum-snapshot-asserted untouched. bash-3.2-safe (no mapfile/assoc arrays/`;&`; python3-datetime timestamps instead of `stat`/`date` flavor probes; harness heredoc written to a file first — bash 3.2 mis-parses `$(…)` around a heredoc body containing an apostrophe). Zero behavior change to `send-telemetry-core.sh` — no bug was exposed; the email-regex over-match remains an accepted, documented trade-off. Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 57 skills / 21 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.

**v15.2.1 — "Which command?" decision table + flag-table completeness audit (docs-only):** A user who is not the maintainer can now answer "which command do I run?" in one screen. **(1) Decision table:** adds a consistent "Which command?" table to `README.md` and `.claude-plugin/README.md` (Commands sections) plus a condensed version in `commands/agent-help.md`, mapping the five common intents — new task/goal → `/launch-pad` + `/supervisor` (or `/autonomous` for the chained loop); multi-iteration on one goal with stacked PRs → `/autonomous`; queue of independent goals (folder/backlog) → `/automate`; existing PR to review-and-heal → `/review-pr`; review-only of a diff → `/code-reviewer` — with a per-row "what it does NOT do" note (none merge except the opt-in, default-OFF `/automate --auto-merge` gate). **(2) Flag-table completeness audit** of `commands/supervisor.md` / `autonomous.md` / `automate.md`: every real flag verified against a Parameters-table row with default + "only meaningful when" precondition (missing defaults added to `--single-iteration` / `--no-stacked-branches` / `--notify` / `--non-interactive-fallback` / `--cheap` rows in `autonomous.md`; foreign flags like `--squash` / `--base` / `--json` / `--agent` and the explicitly-deferred `autonomous.md` "Not shipped" set excluded by rationale). `commands/supervisor.md` now makes `--until-mergeable`'s ownership explicit (a `/review-pr` flag — Supervisor only threads the signal to the dispatched runner via env vars). `commands/autonomous.md`'s `--cheap` interaction note now enumerates the complete forward set with per-flag conditions, matching `skills/autonomous-loop/SKILL.md` §"Auto-forwarded flags" exactly, and gains the iteration-vs-item terminology note (iteration ≈ one `/launch-pad`→`/supervisor` pass on the SAME goal; `/automate`'s "item" = one independent goal from a queue). `automate.md` unchanged beyond audit verification — the 5 trusted-merge conditions remain un-restated (skill §10 stays the authority) and the merge-executor invariant grep still resolves to exactly the 4 sanctioned surfaces. Docs-only; no behavior change. **Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 57 skills / 21 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.2.0.

**v15.2.0 — `--cheap` cost-profile passthrough through `/automate → /autonomous → /supervisor` (flag-plumbing + docs, additive):** Makes `--cheap` a real, forwarded flag on `/autonomous` and `/automate` so the Sonnet cost profile propagates through the full nesting chain. Previously `/supervisor --cheap` was fully wired but `/autonomous` documented `--cheap` as an inert no-op and `/automate` had no cost-profile handling. **(1) `/autonomous` forwarding (`skills/autonomous-loop/SKILL.md` + `commands/autonomous.md`):** INIT parses `--cheap` as run state; EXECUTE step 1 appends it to **every** inlined `/supervisor job:` invocation — unconditional-when-present across single/multi-iteration and stacked/non-stacked, NOT gated on `--non-interactive-fallback`. The previously-dangling "Auto-forwarded flags" subsection is now DEFINED in EXECUTE step 1, enumerating the complete forward set (`--base-branch` for stacked iter>1, `--non-interactive` under `--non-interactive-fallback`, `--cheap` when passed at INIT) — also correcting the pre-existing "forwards ONLY `--non-interactive`" inaccuracy. The two stale "not forwarded / no effect" `--cheap` notes in `commands/autonomous.md` are rewritten; the `--skip-preflight-sync` note no longer claims `--cheap` parity (that flag remains unforwarded). **(2) `/automate` passthrough (`skills/automate-loop/SKILL.md` + `commands/automate.md`):** the §6 step 2 RUN invocation appends `--cheap` to the inner `/autonomous --single-iteration --requirement <path>` call when passed to `/automate` — **pure passthrough** (the engine never interprets it; NOT persisted in the run file's `## Run Config`, same convention as `--notify` / `--non-interactive-fallback` — re-pass on `--resume` / `/loop` ticks); added to the §11 passthrough enumeration, the §12 invocation examples, and the Quality Gates list. **(3) Supervisor cost-profile engine UNCHANGED** — INIT parse → `cost_profile=cheap` → Sonnet overrides on execution-shaped spawns is reused as-is; the Haiku-session caveat stays single-sourced in `docs/ARCHITECTURE_CONTRACTS.md` §"Cost Profiles". Strictly additive and opt-in — without the flag both loops are byte-for-byte unchanged. The CLAUDE.md `### /autonomous --cheap is unsupported in v1` pitfall is rewritten to reflect supported forwarding. **Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 57 skills / 21 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.1.0.

**v15.1.0 — advisory house-rules ENFORCEMENT wired at 3 seams + `/rules add`/`check` mechanized (slice #3b-ii, additive):** Completes the `.agent/rules/` House Rules slice that v14.51.0 opened as substrate-only. This slice adds the ADVISORY enforcement half — the reader is now consumed at three seams, and the write/execute paths are mechanized into tested helpers — while keeping every existing invariant (advisory, fail-safe, never-gating, subordinate to CLAUDE.md).

- **(1) Advisory enforcement at 3 seams (never-gating, mirroring `prior_churn` / `read-postmortem.sh`):**
  - **Worker DO-side.** `agents/supervisor.md` (fast-path worker spawn) and `agents/execute-manager.md` (parallel-path worker spawn) run `read-rules.sh <touched paths...>` (args, never stdin — no-hang) and inject its output into the worker prompt ONLY when NON-EMPTY, as advisory implementation context. House rules never fail a worker, are never a `SUPERVISOR_RESULT` field, and never bump `schema_version`.
  - **Phase 4.5 self-heal REVIEW lens.** `agents/supervisor.md` step 1e (sibling to the `prior_churn`/bridge steps 1c/1d) + `skills/self-heal-advisory/SKILL.md` run `read-rules.sh` on the integrated-diff touched-file scope and thread a **HOUSE-RULES ADVISORY** line into the `code-reviewer` Task prompt (only when non-empty) — biasing WHERE the reviewer looks, never WHETHER the diff passes. It NEVER changes the review `decision` or `heal_decision`, never drives the fix task, and never gates the PR.
  - **SessionStart nudge.** Folded into `scripts/session-resume.sh` (hook-neutral — no new hook entry), gated on the sibling `read-rules.sh` emitting NON-EMPTY stdout (never on bare file presence). Fail-safe: the reader always exits 0.
- **(2) `/rules add` mechanized** into the sole-writer `scripts/add-rule.sh` — the §7 write discipline in CODE: slug the `category` to a single `[a-z0-9-]` path segment and REJECT (abort non-zero, diagnostic, never silently sanitize) any `/`, `..`, leading-dot, shell-metachar, or empty category so the write can NEVER escape `.agent/rules/`; value-validate before writing; array-only parse-gate (`jq -e 'type=="array"'`, abort-not-clobber); deterministic unique id with `-N` collision suffix across the merged set; provenance stamping via `jq -n --arg`; atomic temp-file + `mv`; read-back verify; confirm-only. Proven by `scripts/test-add-rule.sh` (rejects `../escape`, `a/b`, `.hidden`, `foo;rm -rf`, backtick, and empty categories with no traversal write). `/rules add` in `commands/rules.md` is now a thin caller.
- **(3) `/rules check` mechanized** into `scripts/rules-check.sh` — the ONLY execution path in the slice. It reads the store directly via jq for the byte-exact `.check` string (reusing the reader's per-object validation + `LC_ALL=C` first-seen-`id`-wins dedup), runs ONLY `must` rules with a non-null string `check` from the repo root via `bash -c`, DISPLAYS each before running, and executes ONLY behind an explicit confirmation gate (TTY / `--confirm` / `RULES_CHECK_CONFIRM=1`). A default-off **`--no-cmd` unattended trust valve** (mirroring `run-ground-truth.sh --no-cmd`) skips ALL execution and **WINS over `--confirm`** if both are passed. Proven by `scripts/test-rules-check.sh`. No advisory seam ever calls it with execution enabled, and the reader still never executes a `check`.
- **(4) New static trust-boundary self-test** `scripts/test-rules-seams.sh` asserts each of the FOUR advisory seams (`agents/supervisor.md`, `agents/execute-manager.md`, `skills/self-heal-advisory/SKILL.md`, `scripts/session-resume.sh`) references `read-rules.sh`, NEVER references `rules-check.sh`, and NEVER pipes/substitutes/execs `read-rules.sh` OUTPUT into a shell executor. `test-rules-docs.sh` updated to assert the new implemented+gated contract.
- **Doc-currency de-blind:** `scripts/check-doc-currency.sh`'s stale `AI agents vX.Y.Z` headline regex (left over from the rebrand, which renamed the description headline to `Loomwright vX.Y.Z`) is fixed to `Loomwright vX.Y.Z`, so the description headline is gate-guarded again across the 9 doc surfaces.
- `applies_to` path/scope filtering remains **inert** (a later slice); company-base ↔ per-project layering and Tier-3 learned conventions remain deferred.
- **Counts UNCHANGED: 14 agents / 21 commands / 57 skills / 21 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v15.0.0.

**v15.0.0 — Loomwright / atelier naming baseline (mechanical):** Establishes the plugin id `loomwright` (directory + `plugin.json` `name`), the marketplace id `atelier` (`.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` `name`), the persistent-memory directory convention (`.claude/agent-memory/loomwright:<agent>/`), the `-runner` registered-agent IDs, the GitHub repo URL (`github.com/vikashruhilgit/loomwright`), and the `LOOMWRIGHT_*` environment-variable prefix (25 vars — documented inline throughout README, e.g. "Enabling notifications" and "Telemetry"). **No functional/behavioral change** — every agent, command, skill, hook, and gate is byte-identical in logic; mechanical release only. **Counts unchanged: 14 agents / 21 commands / 57 skills / 21 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.**

**v14.51.0 — new `/rules` House Rules substrate command (advisory, additive):** Adds ONE new slash command (`commands/rules.md`) and ONE new skill (`skills/rules/`) that establish a committed, project-local **House Rules store** under `.agent/rules/` — durable team conventions agents can surface but that never gate or execute. `/rules` supports **list / suggest / add / check** verbs over the store, backed by a fail-safe reader `scripts/read-rules.sh` (silently skips an absent store, always exits 0) with self-tests `scripts/test-read-rules.sh` and `scripts/test-rules-docs.sh` (auto-registered by the `ci.yml` `test-*.sh` glob). **Substrate only** — this slice ships the store, command, skill, and reader; **enforcement is deferred to slice #3b-ii**. **Advisory / fail-safe / never-executes-a-check** — it reads and reports conventions but never blocks a PR, changes a `heal_decision`, or runs anything. **One new command (count 20 → 21) and one new skill (count 56 → 57); agent / hook counts unchanged at 14 agents / 21 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.50.0.

**v14.50.0 — new `/setup twin` cold-start bootstrap module (advisory, additive):** Adds a 7th MODULE to the existing `/setup` umbrella command — a sub-module, **NOT** a new top-level command — that bootstraps a repo into a Twin-ready state, mirroring the `/setup observability` module contract (check → report → offer → apply → verify; idempotent, never blind-overwrite). **(1) Twin-readiness status:** `/setup twin` reports whether the repo has a code graph (`graphify-out/graph.json`), a findings→community bridge (`.supervisor/bridge/bridge.json`), and a project `CLAUDE.md`. **(2) Guided cold-start bootstrap:** offers to build the missing pieces — the bridge and a starter `CLAUDE.md` directly, and the code graph when the external `graphify` CLI is available (otherwise it *guides* you to run `/graphify .`, never hard-depending on the external tool) — so a fresh clone can reach the Twin substrate the plugin's advisory area-knowledge (Launch Pad risk + Phase 4.5 self-heal) consumes. **(3) Mechanizable engine + self-test:** the module is implemented by `scripts/setup-twin.sh` (the engine) with a fixture-driven self-test `scripts/test-setup-twin.sh` (green in CI; auto-registered by the `ci.yml` `test-*.sh` glob). **Advisory / fail-safe / idempotent / scoped-writes** — it never blind-overwrites, never gates or blocks, and confines writes to the bootstrap targets. **A `/setup` sub-module is NOT a new command/skill/agent/hook — counts unchanged at 14 agents / 20 commands / 56 skills / 21 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.49.0.

**v14.49.0 — new `/handoff` catch-up / hand-off digest command (read-only, additive):** Adds ONE new slash command (`commands/handoff.md`) plus its deterministic assembler `scripts/build-handoff.sh` and a fixture-driven self-test `scripts/test-build-handoff.sh` (auto-registered by the `ci.yml` `test-*.sh` glob). `/handoff` assembles **ONE recency-focused, per-work-item catch-up digest** so a second person can pick up where you left off in ~2 minutes: it interleaves work items across **Supervisor jobs**, **autonomous runs**, and **automate runs** into a single newest-first, **mode-agnostic** list, surfacing the five facets where derivable — **decision · why · tried/rejected · current state · provenance** (the source artifact path) — plus an honest **freshness / basis line** that **never conflates** artifact mtime with a recorded commit-SHA (a SHA comparison against `HEAD` appears only when a structured trailer recorded one; otherwise basis is mtime and freshness is reported `unknown`). It **reuses the existing sanctioned readers** (`read-project-memory.sh` / `read-lessons.sh`) instead of re-parsing those stores, **silently skips absent surfaces** (and emits a benign "nothing to summarize yet" line when there is no continuity surface at all), writes a derived digest to `.supervisor/handoff/digest.md` (gitignored), echoes the path, and **always exits 0**. **Read-only / fail-safe** — touches no code, no agent, no state-of-truth surface, sends nothing anywhere, and never gates or blocks. Distinct from `/insights` (run trends / aggregates) and `/obsidian` (external vault projection). **One new command (count 19 → 20); agent / skill / hook counts unchanged at 14 agents / 56 skills / 21 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.48.0.

**v14.48.0 — consolidate the three "verify-before-you-assert" lessons into ONE named Read-Before-Write Verification Gate (advisory, additive):** Folds three recurring incident-derived lessons (verify file existence with a second tool before claiming "missing"; verify a consumer's match/filter + required fields before claiming "for free"/"X-compatible"; verify exact command/API/dispatch/spawn shapes from the actual file before asserting them) into ONE named **Read-Before-Write Verification Gate** rule in `AGENT_GUIDELINES.md`, plus a matching pre-write gate item in the preloaded `quality-checklist` skill (bumped 1.1.0 → 1.2.0, `SKILLS_INDEX.md` row synced). **Advisory / non-gating** — the gate is guidance every agent inherits via the standard contract, not a new hook or hard gate; `check-doc-currency.sh` + `validate-version.sh` green. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 19 commands / 56 skills / 21 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.47.0.

**v14.47.0 — per-project OpenTelemetry resource-attribute labeling on session start (telemetry-gated, additive; first hook-count bump since v14.34.0):** Adds ONE new `SessionStart` hook (`scripts/set-otel-resource-attrs.sh`, `type: command`) that auto-maintains each project's `OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES` (`service.name=<repo>`, `service.version=<plugin version>`) inside `<project>/.claude/settings.local.json` so every project's OpenTelemetry traces are labeled with the originating repo + the active plugin version. **Telemetry-gated:** the hook self-gates on telemetry being enabled (no-op otherwise) and is **fail-safe — ALWAYS exits 0** (never blocks session start, never throws on a malformed/absent settings file, never writes outside `<project>/.claude/settings.local.json`). The `/setup observability` flow owns the label lifecycle (init / status / remove); `setup.md`, the setup SKILL, and `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md` prose document the new label. Ships with a fixture-driven self-test (`scripts/test-set-otel-resource-attrs.sh`, auto-registered by the `ci.yml` `test-*.sh` glob). The hooks.json wiring brings the leaf count **20 → 21**; `check-doc-currency.sh` + `validate-version.sh` green. **One new hook (count 20 → 21); agent / command / skill counts unchanged at 14 agents / 19 commands / 56 skills; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.46.0.

**v14.46.0 — findings→community bridge wired as advisory area-knowledge into Phase 4.5 self-heal + Launch Pad risk (Local Twin Step 5, additive):** Graduates the proven Step-4 spike into shipped tooling and wires it as advisory, non-gating, fail-safe context. **(1) Bridge builder (`scripts/build-bridge.py` engine + `scripts/build-bridge.sh` wrapper, mirroring the `measure-heal-signal` graduation):** rebuilds a per-graph-community index from the current `graphify-out/graph.json` + findings corpora (postmortem results, heal-signal joins, `LESSONS.md`), emitting a gitignored `.supervisor/bridge/bridge.json` whose load-bearing `file_to_communities` map is keyed by the RAW repo-root-relative `source_file` (with the `loomwright/` prefix — byte-identical to `git diff --name-only`, never `strip_prefix`-ed) and a `communities` DICT keyed by str id for O(1) reader lookup; repo identity is derived machine-agnostically from the git remote (no hard-coded slug; fail-open when no remote); READ-ONLY toward inputs; exits 0 on missing graph/python (jq is NOT a builder dependency). **(2) Runtime reader (`scripts/read-bridge.sh`):** args→bounded advisory markdown on a touched-path→community hit, EMPTY on no-hit, silent exit 0 on absent bridge/graph/jq, with a staleness "treat as hint" caveat when HEAD≠`built_at_commit` (stale = emit-with-hint, NOT a silent no-op), and god-node (ubiquitous-community) suppression so output is SPECIFIC area knowledge; jq-only / injection-safe; ALWAYS exits 0. **(3) Fixture-driven self-test (`scripts/test-build-bridge.sh`, auto-registered by the `ci.yml` `test-*.sh` glob):** covers path-form join, machine-agnostic repo-slug derivation, lessons-text ingestion, god-node suppression, and stale-emits-with-hint. **(4) Wiring:** Phase 4.5 self-heal (`agents/supervisor.md` step 1d sibling to step 1c + `skills/self-heal-advisory/SKILL.md`) and Launch Pad risk (`agents/launch-pad.md` Phase 3 consult + Phase 5 risk row) call `read-bridge.sh` UNCONDITIONALLY (self-gates on bridge.json, mirroring `read-postmortem.sh`); the community area-knowledge feeds the self-heal REVIEW lens only (never workers/fixers), threaded into the `code-reviewer` prompt as a non-empty-guarded AREA-KNOWLEDGE ADVISORY line ("bias WHERE you look, not WHETHER the diff passes"). The shared seam is documented in `skills/brain-context/SKILL.md` ("Bridge read"). **Invariants intact:** advisory / non-gating / fail-safe throughout — the bridge NEVER changes `heal_decision`, a review `decision`, the GO/CAUTION/NO-GO verdict, or any gate, and NEVER blocks a PR or brief save; a repo with no graph/bridge is entirely unaffected (silent no-op); the bridge community read counts under the EXISTING `brain_context` tag in `knowledge_sources_used` (no new tag). **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 19 commands / 56 skills / 20 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.45.0.

**v14.45.0 — maintenance version bump to force the hosted-marketplace re-index (no functional change):** Identical plugin surface to v14.44.0 — bumped solely to publish a fresh version after the desktop hosted-marketplace registry stalled on the 14.43.0→14.44.0 index (a desktop uninstall/reinstall kept resolving 14.43.0 even though `origin/main` carried 14.44.0). No code, agent, command, skill, or hook changes; the two manifests and every current-claim `(vX.Y.Z)` annotation advance to 14.45.0. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 19 commands / 56 skills / 20 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.44.0.

**v14.44.0 — `/automate` emits an honest engine-native ground-truth churn line (no more GitHub-blind false-0 postmortem) (additive):** Fixes `/automate` reading `review_rounds: 0` on exactly the flow it produces (inline `/review-pr --until-mergeable` drain + CI-check review + squash-merge) — the `/pr-postmortem` gather is GitHub-surface and the drain's review never reaches GitHub, so all three of its signals read 0. Instead of patching the generic postmortem, the engine now emits its OWN truthful line from data it ALREADY holds. **(1) Fail-safe `learning-emit` helper (`scripts/automate-helpers.sh` + self-tests):** a new `learning-emit` subcommand builds ONE full valid `schema_version: 1` POSTMORTEM_RESULT (jq-only / injection-safe, `set +e` always-exit-0 like `send-webhook.sh`) from the owned drain's `REVIEW_HEAL_RESULT` (`fix_cycles`/`repeat_check_failure`/`unresolved_bot_feedback`/`decision`) + the inner `SUPERVISOR_RESULT` (`repo`/`number`/`pr_url`/`branch`) plus a single `gh pr view --json files,additions,deletions,changedFiles`. `review_rounds` = `effective_review_rounds` (= drain `fix_cycles`, or `1` for a zero-cycle escalation); a single honestly-labeled synthetic `categories[]` entry (`drain_churn` / `drain_escalation`) honors the **zero-rule** (`categories: []` only when `effective_review_rounds == 0`, so the live `read-postmortem.sh` reader never sees fake churn); `changed_paths` is populated and a real `repo` is passed (both load-bearing — the reader matches on `changed_paths` overlap AND `repo`); a deterministic `automate_key` (`run_id`+item+`pr_url`+`source`) makes a crash/`--resume` re-entry idempotent (exactly one line). **(2) Wired at end-of-DRAIN (`skills/automate-loop/SKILL.md` §6 step 3):** emitted AFTER the terminal `REVIEW_HEAL_RESULT` read and BEFORE the GATE, so PARKED items (which stop at the GATE and never reach CHECK OFF) are covered too — one line per PR, merged OR parked. The emit is **advisory / fail-SAFE — its exit status is ignored and it NEVER changes `owned_drain_result`, the GATE, `## Status`, `## Current`, or any `/autonomous` gate** (mirrors `postmortem_dispatched`-is-informational). **(3) `--no-auto-postmortem` on the owned drain (§7):** the engine's owned `/review-pr --until-mergeable` now runs with `--no-auto-postmortem` so the corpus gets ONE honest engine-native line, not one honest + one GitHub-blind false-0 (the standalone `/review-pr` tail OUTSIDE automate is unchanged). **(4) Additive schema (`docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md`):** two optional additive POSTMORTEM_RESULT fields — `source` (`"github_postmortem"` implicit default \| `"automate_drain"`) and `automate_key` — with a worked `automate_drain` variant note; **no `schema_version` bump** (same precedent as `pr_url`/`changed_paths`). **Invariants intact:** `pr-postmortem-gather.sh` is untouched (stays a generic GitHub-surface analyzer); the sole `gh pr merge --squash` executor and `/automate` single-drain ownership are unchanged; runtime emitters fail-SAFE (exit 0) while correctness gates fail-CLOSED. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 19 commands / 56 skills / 20 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.43.0.

**v14.43.0 — first-line review rigor (quality, not pass-the-PR): adversarial-input + execution-grounded lens in the shared reviewer, a correctness⇒HIGH/BLOCKING severity rule, and observable anticipatory fix-worker self-review (additive):** Makes the plugin's first-line code review actually *catch real defects* (negative-amount exploits, idempotency holes, run-once violations) instead of reading a checklist — measured by defects caught early / escaped to `main`, **not** reviewer agreement or gate-pass rate — without homogenizing the two independent review lenses or weakening the **#64** lesson. **(1) Rigor in the shared `code-reviewer` brain (`agents/code-reviewer.md` + the preloaded `skills/quality-checklist/SKILL.md` Self-Heal Miss-Class Checklist):** two new Review-Process steps — an **Adversarial-Input Lens** (interrogate every changed code path for negative/zero/overflow/empty, replay-idempotency, and concurrency) and an **Execution-Grounded Verification** step that runs ONLY non-mutating checks (type-check; the specific tests covering the diff) and NEVER runs update/fix/format/snapshot-update/coverage/migration/seed commands. When behavior can't be verified it is reported **`unverified` (never a silent pass)**; a **load-bearing** unverified correctness/security behavior returns `NEEDS_HUMAN`/blocking (a non-load-bearing one is reported but non-blocking). Because the reviewer is a **shared brain**, Phase 4.5 self-heal, the `/review-pr` drain, and standalone `/code-reviewer` all inherit the rigor with no per-caller duplication. **(2) Severity-assignment rule (closes the inert-lens gap):** any confirmed correctness/security/behavior regression introduced by the diff MUST be labeled **HIGH or BLOCKING** (never MEDIUM/LOW) so the diff-review fix floor (`new` + BLOCKING/HIGH) actually fixes it — MEDIUM/LOW stay for maintainability/polish. This is **NOT** a drain severity floor (the #64 anti-pattern stays pinned) and does **NOT** touch the `drift` severity caps. **(3) Observable anticipatory fix-worker self-review (`agents/supervisor.md` Phase 4.5 fixer + both `skills/review-heal/SKILL.md` fix prompts):** before pushing, every fix worker re-reads its OWN diff for downstream regressions (persistence/state/lifecycle/idempotency/concurrency — the class behind combo-split→double-`Save` and earn-counter→dedup-collision) and emits an observable `self_review:` note; a push with no note is treated as incomplete by a co-located gate on all three fixer sites. **(4) Drain cosmetic-defer DEFERRED by evidence:** the original problem also aimed to stop the `--until-mergeable` drain grinding on cosmetic noise, but the postmortems of two PRs on a private downstream repo showed the churn was a *real self-heal miss* (#31 `self_heal_misses=1`), not cosmetic noise — so **no drain cosmetic-defer semantics ship this run** (READY / remaining_issues / findings_* untouched; no `findings_deferred` field); parked for a follow-up brief. **Invariants intact:** the two review lenses stay independent (no rubric-mirroring); `/review-pr` & Phase 4.5 still NEVER merge; the sole `gh pr merge --squash` executor is unchanged; runtime emitters fail-SAFE (exit 0) while correctness gates fail-CLOSED. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 19 commands / 56 skills / 20 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.42.0.

**v14.42.0 — worktree-isolate the detached until-mergeable review drain + retire the suppress-for-the-self-heal-race kill-switch:** Ends the four-PR until-mergeable review-drain dispatch saga (#67/#70/#71/#72) by removing the **root hazard** instead of adding a fifth dispatch guarantee. **The problem:** the detached `review-pr-runner` drain ran in the SAME working tree/index as the inline Phase 4.5 self-heal (`git checkout "$HEAD_REF"` in the shared checkout), so each reliability fix kept re-exposing the collision → an operator suppressed it by writing `.supervisor/config.json {"auto_review": false}` → the kill-switch had no safe restore → a genuine drain (PR #74) was silently dropped (no marker, `until_mergeable_dispatched: false`). **(1) Isolated worktree for the detached dispatched drain (`scripts/dispatch-pr-review.sh`):** the dispatcher now runs the detached drain in its OWN git worktree — a plugin-owned **sibling** of the primary checkout (`../{project}-review-{pr_hash}`, OUTSIDE the tracked tree so an inline `git add -A` can never sweep it), created via a **detached-HEAD checkout at the PR head SHA** (`git worktree add --detach <path> <sha>`) so a same-branch drain never fails with "branch already checked out". It never checks-out / stages / commits in the inline session's working tree. **Scope note:** isolation targets only the *detached dispatched* path (the one concurrent with self-heal); a user running `/review-pr` inline in their own session has no concurrent self-heal and stays worktree-free. **(2) Atomic claim + stale-lock reclaim + marker-after-success (the pinned AC4a order):** ① existing-marker-wins `exit 0` → ② verify `claude` launchable, else `exit 0` with **no marker** → ③ atomic `mkdir "<pr_hash>.lock"` (loser logs one line and exits 0; lock records `pr_url`/`pid`/`ts`/paths) → ④ create the sibling worktree → ⑤ write the RUN_LOG header → ⑥ write the durable marker → ⑦ launch the detached wrapper. The **marker is written only after** the worktree+header succeed, so a marker can never claim a dispatch that did not actually start (the AC5 audit depends on this). **Stale-lock reclaim** clears a wedged lock ONLY when ALL hold — no durable marker, recorded `pid` not alive, and lock `ts` older than a conservative TTL — closing the crash-between-`mkdir`-and-marker permanent-wedge without inheriting the old marker-as-claim behavior. **(3) Executable trap-owned cleanup + fork-aware push:** terminal/crash cleanup is owned by a **`trap cleanup EXIT` wrapper process** (not a prompt-only "remove on exit" instruction the agent can skip — the very failure mode this saga is about) that removes ONLY the dispatcher-created, hash-keyed worktree+lock (no broad `*-review-*` glob sweep); same-repo pushes use an explicit refspec (`git push origin HEAD:<head_ref>`, **never `--force`**); cross-repo/fork PRs degrade to **review-only `ESCALATED`** rather than pushing to the wrong ref. **(4) Retire the race rationale across all surfaces:** the three byte-identical "no worktree fan-out" claims (`skills/review-heal/SKILL.md`, `commands/review-pr.md`, `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md`) are reconciled to the SAME scoped wording (inline session = no fan-out; detached dispatched drain = its own dispatcher-created sibling worktree); the `agents/supervisor.md` Phase 4.5 step-5.5 timing note no longer relies on "concurrent-with-self-heal is safe-by-degradation"; and **no repo guidance recommends writing `auto_review:false` to suppress the drain for the self-heal race** (the config flag itself REMAINS as a general opt-out — only the suppress-for-the-race *rationale* is retired). **(5) Missing-drain reconciliation in `/insights` (`scripts/build-insights.sh`):** a new advisory **`## Missing-drain reconciliation`** section surfaces PRs that carry a heal outcome but have NO dispatch marker, with a reason classification — `opted_out` ONLY on durable run-time-recorded opt-out evidence (never inferred from the *current* mutable `config.json`), else `unknown_or_opted_out` (the default; a genuine silent drop like #74 surfaces here as the signal to investigate). Read-only, advisory, SUPPRESSED entirely when no heal-signal/marker corpus exists (no fabricated zeros). **(6) Invariants preserved:** all dispatch paths still ALWAYS `exit 0` / fail-safe (never gate, never block the PR, never change `heal_decision`); the drain still NEVER merges and NEVER `--force`-pushes; the audit is advisory-only; the existing two dispatch paths + idempotency marker are unchanged (this REMOVES a failure mode, it does not add a fifth dispatch path). **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 19 commands / 56 skills / 20 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.41.0.

**v14.41.0 — `/automate` generic automation engine (single run file → per-item loop):** A new `/automate` command + `automate-loop` authority skill that walks **arbitrary work from any starting point** to a reviewed PR (and, opt-in, a trusted merge). **The problem:** the plugin could drive ONE requirement deep (`/autonomous`) but nothing walked a whole backlog. **The shape:** intake is source-agnostic — only the *intake* differs, and intake is just "convert the source into the run file's Queue, once" (NOT a pluggable adapter framework). **(1) Intake — any source → the Queue (convert once):** `/automate "X"` runs `/product-owner` on the prompt (REUSE — PO writes `.supervisor/requirements/*.md` story files in Beads-absent mode) and the generated paths become the Queue; `--folder <dir>` enqueues every `.md` not `## Status: done`; `--backlog <_BACKLOG.md>` enqueues in documented dependency order. `/backlog` is **NOT** a separate command — a folder/backlog-doc is just one kind of source. `--limit N` (default 5) caps **PROCESSED** items, not Queue size — the full resolved Queue is materialized and **human-confirmed before processing** (a prompt that explodes into 40 files never runs silently). **(2) The single-file model (this design's core):** there is **no manifest, no registry, no progress.jsonl, no dashboard file** — ONE markdown run file `.supervisor/automate/<run_id>.md` holds Status/Source/Run Config/Queue/Current/append-only Progress and is the contract, dashboard, and resume state; "find prior runs" = glob `.supervisor/automate/*.md` not `## Status: done`. Crash-safe: **atomic write (temp + rename)**, `## Progress` append-only, rewrites confined to `## Queue` checkboxes + `## Current`. **(3) Smart resume = glob + reconcile:** the run file is the loop's *belief*; on every start it reconciles each in-flight item against **ground truth** (`gh pr view` merged?, `git branch --contains`, requirement `## Status: done`) BEFORE trusting a checkbox (a crash between merge and check-off makes them disagree), then offers continue / start-new / archive; ambiguous resume fails **closed** under `--non-interactive-fallback` (`resume_ambiguous_non_interactive`). **(4) Per-item loop:** RECONCILE → `/autonomous --single-iteration --requirement <path>` → own **exactly ONE** inline `/review-pr --until-mergeable` drain → GATE → SYNC (pull `main`) → check off + append Progress. **Single-drain ownership:** the loop sets `.supervisor/config.json {"auto_review": false}` around the inner `/autonomous` RUN phase (byte-for-byte backup to a transient `<run_id>.config-backup.json` + absent-delete + malformed-abort + RECONCILE crash-restore) so Supervisor's default detached drain (step 5.5 + the `PostToolUse[Bash]` hook) does not double-dispatch against the engine's own drain. **(5) Two modes + the trusted-merge gate:** `READY` means "ready, left open for a human" — **nothing in the plugin merges** by default (safe mode parks `awaiting_merge`); opt-in `--auto-merge` (default OFF) executes `gh pr merge --squash` ONLY behind a **5-condition fail-closed gate** (drain `READY` + head-SHA-unchanged & base `main` + `reviewDecision` ∉ {`CHANGES_REQUESTED`,`REVIEW_REQUIRED`} with a GraphQL-only unresolved-human-thread check + enforceable branch protection or `--trust-unprotected` + required-checks-green-AND-rubric-satisfied from `SUPERVISOR_RESULT.rubric_score`). `ESCALATED` never merges and **parks** the run; the **single-open-PR invariant** holds in BOTH modes (never pick a new item while one has an open unmerged PR). **(6) Invariant preservation:** `review-heal` / Supervisor Phase 4.5 still NEVER merge — the `automate-loop` `--auto-merge` gate is now the **only** sanctioned executor of `gh pr merge --squash` in the plugin (recorded as a durable CLAUDE.md Failure-Mode Invariant, not just this banner). **(7) Schema + tests:** `AUTOMATE_RUN` documented at `schema_version: 1` as a **markdown state-file contract, NOT a hook-validated emitted result block** (nothing validates it at a SubagentStop); helpers (`scripts/automate-helpers.sh`) + **50 stubbed-`gh` self-tests** (`scripts/test-automate-helpers.sh`: config suppress/restore incl. absent-delete & malformed-abort, `config-orig` true/false/absent/malformed-abort, atomic run-file write + append-only Progress incl. create-section, queue check-off idempotency + `abandoned` mark, resume reconcile belief-vs-truth, auto-merge fail-closed on each of the 5 blockers incl. null/absent-rubric, the reviews-not-required reachability paths (checks-only / `--trust-unprotected` ⇒ MERGE), the fail-CLOSED unresolved-human-thread path (missing/null ⇒ PARK), + all-pass-merge-fires-once). Driven continuously by Claude's `/loop /loomwright:automate`. **New: 1 command (`/automate`), 1 skill (`automate-loop`); no new agent / hook / runner (`/automate` is inline-only) — counts move to 14 agents / 19 commands / 56 skills / 20 hooks; no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.40.0.

**v14.40.0 — graduate the Local Twin self-heal catch-rate measurement from a scratch spike into a shipped, re-runnable instrument + wire it into `/insights`:** Step 2b of the Local Twin path (`docs/SPIKES/LOCAL_TWIN_PATH.md` §1 Step 2) turns the throwaway Step-1 measurement into a permanent dial — "what is the self-heal catch-rate?" is now re-runnable anytime, across repos, instead of a one-off scratch script. **(1) The tool (`scripts/measure-heal-signal.{sh,py}`, uncounted + `scripts/test-measure-heal-signal.sh`):** a READ-ONLY instrument that HARVESTs the heal signal from done-brief `## Outcome` blocks → LOADs the `/pr-postmortem` churn labels → JOINs on PR URL → emits the **same confusion matrix (recall / false-positive / false-negative) + JSONL output shape as the Step-1 spike** (`signal.jsonl`, `labels.jsonl`, `joined.jsonl`, `_summary.json`, `report.md`). The Step-1 logic is PRESERVED, not reinvented — the spike's HARVEST/JOIN/MATRIX is lifted verbatim and generalized. The `.py` is the engine; the `.sh` is a thin CLI wrapper that resolves the repo list and output dir. **The repo list is parameterized** (precedence: `--repo`/`--repos` flags > `$LOOMWRIGHT_HEAL_SIGNAL_REPOS` > `.supervisor/config.json .measure_heal_signal.repos` > default = the current git repo). The default is machine-agnostic and reproduces the Step-1 baseline because the join only ever resolves within the self repo (other repos contribute harvested signal but zero joined labels); to reproduce the Step-1 6-repo HARVEST, pass those paths. **READ-ONLY toward measured repos** (it only READs their `.supervisor/jobs/done/*.md` + `.supervisor/postmortem/results.jsonl`); it WRITES only under `--out` (default `.supervisor/heal-signal/`, gitignored). **(2) Make the counts less of a floor:** `review_rounds` / `self_heal_misses` are documented FLOORS — the churn counter under-counts review feedback that flows through CI-check channels (e.g. PR #47 first logged 0 rounds, a re-gather logged 6) and the gather's review-fix regex has repo-shape gaps. The tool's label dedup now takes the **per-field MAX across all re-gathers** (so a corrected-upward re-gather wins) instead of latest-`ts`-only, and surfaces **label-quality diagnostics** (model-guessed / explicit-zero-churn-signal / floor-raised counts) so the floor is VISIBLE in the report rather than silently trusted. Fully closing the gather's regex gaps stays the test-pinned `pr-postmortem-gather.sh` domain — this raises the floor without touching those heuristics. **(3) `/insights` MEASURE leg:** `scripts/build-insights.sh` gains a `## Heal-signal catch-rate (MEASURE)` section sourced from `.supervisor/heal-signal/results.jsonl` (one append-only trend line per run) — latest catch-rate (recall), false-negatives/N, coverage, self-heal-lane churn share, and a recall TREND oldest→newest (bounded ~10 points). Advisory / DIRECTIONAL (churn labels) — never gates a PR or changes a heal decision — and **SUPPRESSED entirely when no scored matrix exists** (n=0 / no ledger), mirroring the System-Twin-hard-signal + knowledge-sources precedent (no fabricated zeros, no "no data yet" stub). **(4) Coverage gap surfaced as a DECISION, not an action:** the matrix sees only PRs carrying BOTH a heal signal AND a model-classified label (~10–24% today). `--backfill N` PLANS the work — lists up to N unlabeled heal-signal PRs (most-recent first) + the estimated wall-clock cost and STOPS. It **NEVER dispatches `/pr-postmortem`** (the label needs full model classification, not just gather), so a ~3-hour bulk backfill can never run by accident; the human decides how many to backfill. **(5) Self-tests:** `test-measure-heal-signal.sh` ships 13 cases (matrix correctness, floor-raising flips TN→FN, bold+lowercase Outcome forms, label-quality diagnostics, backfill-is-a-plan-only, READ-ONLY-invariant byte-identical-tree check, trend-ledger append + `--no-ledger` suppression, fail-safe empty, wrapper default-self + env-override resolution — the last caught a genuine env-split last-line bug in the wrapper); `test-insights.sh` grows to 68 (heal-signal section render with the n=0 record filtered, suppression when only unscored / no-ledger, and a scored-but-recall-null run — tp+fn=0 — rendered with `recall n/a` instead of dropped). Re-running the tool on `loomwright` reproduces the trusted Step-1 baseline at its N=8 coverage (recall 0% / FN 6/8 / self_heal-stage churn share 54%); the **durable signal is recall 0%**, which held when a subsequent human-approved 10-PR `--backfill` round widened self-repo coverage 24%→55% (N 8→18, FN 15/18, self_heal-stage share 53%) — confirming the rubber-stamp is not a small-N artifact. All fail-safe invariants preserved: READ-ONLY toward measured repos, advisory only, always exits 0 on the wrapper's no-python/no-engine paths, never blocks a run or changes a `heal_decision`. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (the tool + tests are uncounted scripts; counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 20 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.39.0.

**v14.39.0 — fix the `until_mergeable_dispatched` observability false-negative + the 0-byte dispatch-log ambiguity:** The until-mergeable review drain's own observability signal could misreport whether it ran on the inline execution path — the exact defect that made a real session claim "not dispatched" while the drain was alive (PID running, marker written, PR already review-commented). Two compounding root causes. **Root cause A — the field was authored from belief, not reconciled against reality:** `until_mergeable_dispatched` (on `SUPERVISOR_RESULT`, the job `## Outcome` block, and the `session_end` JSONL event) was written by Supervisor Phase 4.5 **step 5.5** based solely on whether *that step itself* invoked the dispatcher. But the drain has a second, default-on trigger — the `PostToolUse[Bash]` hook backstop (`hook-dispatch-on-pr-create.sh`, v14.34.0) that fires at `gh pr create`, one phase earlier, **out-of-band and invisible to the main-thread context**. On the inline `/supervisor` & `/autonomous` paths the Supervisor IS the main thread, so when it skips step 5.5 (judging the detached launch best-effort) the hook still dispatches — yet the field recorded `false`.
