Nothing ever reconciled the field against the on-disk dispatch marker that already records the truth. **Root cause B — the dispatch log looked like a failure while succeeding:** the detached `claude -p` runner buffers stdout until exit, so its `RUN_LOG` is a 0-byte file for the entire (minutes-long) drain — indistinguishable from a failed no-op to anyone inspecting it, and the log filename is `shasum(PR_URL)` (not a git SHA), so a naive "SHA doesn't match HEAD" check manufactures a false anomaly. **(1) Fix — synchronous self-documenting log header (`scripts/dispatch-pr-review.sh`):** the dispatcher now writes a non-empty `RUN_LOG` header BEFORE the detached launch — a machine-greppable `DISPATCHED` token plus `ts=`, `url=`, `until_mergeable=` flag, and `runner=`, plus a separate `# marker:` line followed by a plain-language note that an otherwise-empty body below the header means the drain is IN FLIGHT, not failed (liveness: `pgrep -lf review-pr-runner`). An in-flight drain is never again an ambiguous 0-byte file. **(2) Fix — marker reconciliation (`agents/supervisor.md` step 5.5 + the inline `commands/supervisor.md`):** `until_mergeable_dispatched` is now resolved from the per-PR dispatch marker under `.supervisor/review-dispatch/` (exact-match the marker BODY's PR URL — hash-implementation-agnostic, prefix-safe, and independent of the dispatcher's `shasum`/`sha1sum`/sanitized-fallback keying), NOT from "did this step dispatch." A marker exists IFF the dispatcher ran for this PR via EITHER step 5.5 OR the hook backstop, so `true` is truthful regardless of which path fired; `false` is recorded only when no marker exists (opted out / no PR / dispatcher no-op). The inline `commands/supervisor.md` — which previously carried NO `until_mergeable_dispatched` recording directive at all (only `agents/supervisor.md`, the delegated-agent prompt the inline path never loads) — gains the same marker-reconciliation directive, mirrored with step 5.5 as a documented sync pair so the inline and delegated paths never diverge. **(3) Fix — schema doc (`docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md`):** the `until_mergeable_dispatched` definition now states it reflects the marker (dispatched by step 5.5 OR the hook backstop), reconciled against the marker rather than the agent's own action. **(4) Self-test:** `scripts/test-dispatch-pr-review.sh` grows **18 → 20** cases — a real (non-dry-run) launch with a stubbed `claude` asserts the `RUN_LOG` header is present and greppable (`DISPATCHED` + `until_mergeable=1` + `url=`), and the `--no-until-mergeable` opt-out path asserts the header records `until_mergeable=0`; the existing dry-run cases are unchanged (the header is on the real-launch path, after the dry-run short-circuit). All fail-safe invariants are preserved: the header write and the reconciliation are best-effort and never gate, never change a `heal_decision`, and never block the PR or the run. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 20 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.38.0.

**v14.38.0 — fix the inline-path until-mergeable review-drain gap that v14.37.0/#70 left open:** v14.37.0 closed the inline review-drain dispatch on paper, but it put its `state.md` producer write only in the *delegated-agent* prompt (`agents/supervisor.md`) — the prompt loaded only by the `supervisor-runner` subagent. The inline `/supervisor` & `/autonomous` paths run the Supervisor workflow on the **main thread** and read `commands/supervisor.md`, never `agents/supervisor.md`, so on exactly those paths the hook's session-scope gate still found no truthful durable state at `gh pr create` time and the until-mergeable drain stayed silently un-dispatched. This release moves the producer writes into the loaded inline command file and hardens the consumer to survive stale state. **(1) Fix A — load-bearing inline `state.md` write:** the canonical lowercase `## Session` block write (`- status: running`, `- branch:`) plus the Phase 4.5 completion-tail status flip are now ALSO directives in the **loaded** `commands/supervisor.md` Phase 1 ACQUIRE (the file BOTH inline entry points read), mirrored with the existing `agents/supervisor.md` write as a documented sync pair; best-effort and non-fatal, so the durable state the `PostToolUse[Bash]` hook's session-scope gate (term ii status + term iii branch) consumes actually exists and is truthful on the inline paths. **(2) Fix B producer (`/autonomous`-only):** inline ACQUIRE additionally writes two top-level `state.json` ACQUIRE signals — `current_branch` and a non-terminal `current_status: "running"` — explicitly carved out of the `_v1_note` advisory-only rule as deterministic, hook-readable fields (the rest of `state.json` stays advisory/non-contractual). **(3) Fix B consumer (`hook-dispatch-on-pr-create.sh`):** the hook now resolves the active session from ONE coherent source — a non-terminal + branch-matching `.supervisor/state.md`, ELSE a UNIQUE active autonomous `state.json` (branch match + its brief present in `.supervisor/jobs/in-progress/` + non-terminal `current_status` + null `ended_at`) — so a **stale terminal `state.md`** left behind by a prior session no longer short-circuits dispatch ahead of the `state.json` fallback. The gate stays **fail-closed / anti-hijack**: there is still NO current-git-branch fallback, the branch-MATCH requirement and fail-closed-on-unresolvable posture from #67/#70 are preserved, and the hook still ALWAYS `exit 0` so it can never break the originating `gh pr create`. The self-test grows **19 → 29** cases (headline: stale-terminal-`state.md` + valid `state.json` → dispatch; plus stale-`state.json` / multiple-active-`state.json` / wrong-brief / neither-source fail-closed controls, and a Source-1 status-presence guard so a branch-only / status-less `state.md` falls through to the `state.json` fallback instead of authorizing). All fail-safe invariants are preserved: the inline state writes and the hook never gate, never change a `heal_decision`, and never block the PR or the run. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 20 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.37.0.

**v14.37.0 — fix the post-PR review-drain dispatch on the inline `/supervisor` & `/autonomous` paths:** Closes two diagnosed root causes that left the v14.32.0 until-mergeable review drain silently un-dispatched on the inline main-thread execution paths (where the Supervisor IS the main thread, so neither the `SubagentStop(supervisor-runner)` hooks nor a skipped Phase 4.5 prompt step 5.5 ever dispatch it). **(1) Inline canonical `state.md` write:** Phase 1 ACQUIRE and the Phase 4.5 completion tail now write/flip the **canonical lowercase `## Session` block** (`- status:`, `- branch:`) to `.supervisor/state.md` directly on the **inline** path — not only via a Context-Keeper spawn — best-effort and non-fatal, so the durable state the `PostToolUse[Bash]` hook's session-scope gate (term ii status + term iii branch) and `/supervisor --continue` resume both consume actually exists and is truthful at PR-create time. **(2) Hook format-tolerance:** `hook-dispatch-on-pr-create.sh` now parses **both** the canonical lowercase (`- status:` / `- branch:`) **and** the inline-Supervisor **bold** (`- **Status:**` / `- **Branch:**`) `state.md` forms when resolving term (ii) status and term (iii) branch; the anti-hijack branch-MATCH requirement and the fail-closed-on-unresolvable posture are unchanged, the hook still ALWAYS `exit 0`, and its self-test gains bold-format MATCH, branch-MISMATCH, and stale-status cases (19 green). **(3) `/autonomous` single-iteration drain parity:** the autonomous EXECUTE enumeration no longer drops Supervisor **Phase 4.5 step 5.5**, so a single-iteration `/autonomous` run now reaches the post-PR until-mergeable drain through the inline Supervisor workflow — parity with a direct `/supervisor` run; EVALUATE's chained review-heal is documented as an **additional, multi-iteration-only** stacking gate and the R9 downstream-ordering note is unchanged (doc/prompt-level only). All fail-safe invariants are preserved: the hook and the inline state write never gate, never change a `heal_decision`, and never block the PR or the run. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 20 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.36.0.

**v14.36.0 — Learning Loop Phase 4 — churn ledger: postmortem provenance + read-back into Launch Pad & Supervisor Phase 4.5:** Closes the read-back half of the PR-churn learning loop, turning the `/pr-postmortem` trend log into a churn ledger that later planning/review passes can consult. **(1) `POSTMORTEM_RESULT` provenance enrichment:** the result block (and the trend line it writes) gains additive provenance fields — `pr_url`, `brief_path`, `job_path`, `branch`, and `changed_paths` — so a recorded churn finding can be correlated back to the originating brief, job, branch, and the files it touched. Strictly **additive; `POSTMORTEM_RESULT` stays `schema_version: 1`** (the `branch_base`/`pr_state`/`knowledge_sources_used` additive-field precedent). **(2) `read-postmortem.sh` read-back helper:** a new **fail-safe**, read-only helper surfaces prior churn-ledger findings (filterable by changed path) for advisory consumption; on any read miss / missing log / parse failure it degrades to a logged no-op and always exits 0 (per the CLAUDE.md bimodal "side-effect emitters fail SAFE" invariant). **(3) Advisory read-back wiring:** the helper is wired **advisory-only** into **Launch Pad Phase 3 / Risk Assessment** — prior-churn hints for the files a brief touches inform its File Impact Map and Risk Assessment — and into **Supervisor Phase 4.5 pre-review enrichment**, where prior-churn context for the integrated diff primes the self-heal review lens. On every read path the framing is identical: advisory and strictly subordinate to `CLAUDE.md`; reading the ledger MUST NEVER block the run, change a verdict / `heal_decision`, or fail the run; absent/empty ⇒ proceed normally. **(4) Self-test + fixture:** the new helper ships a deterministic self-test plus a fixture trend log (both uncounted `test-*`/fixture scripts). **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 20 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.35.0.

**v14.35.0 — back-compatible rename of `.supervisor/notify-config.json` → `.supervisor/config.json`:** The run-behavior config file is renamed back-compatibly to drop the now-misleading `notify-` prefix (it carries far more than notification settings). **(1) Readers prefer-new-fall-back-to-legacy:** every consumer now reads `.supervisor/config.json` first and falls back to the legacy `.supervisor/notify-config.json`, with the **new path winning when both exist** — so existing installs keep working with zero action and no flag-day migration. **(2) Migration:** users migrate by `mv .supervisor/notify-config.json .supervisor/config.json` or simply rely on the fallback indefinitely. **(3) Doc sweep:** the README current-state setup reference is renamed (with a fallback note) and a migration note is added; dated historical release banners and prior CHANGELOG entries that name `notify-config.json` are left **frozen** (they accurately describe what shipped under the old name and the fallback keeps them working — frozen historical values are not drift). **(4) Version + doc-currency:** version bumped 14.34.0 → 14.35.0 across `plugin.json` / `marketplace.json` / docs; `check-doc-currency.sh` + `validate-version.sh` green. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 20 hooks); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.34.0.

**v14.34.0 — `PostToolUse[Bash]` PR-create backstop for the until-mergeable review drain:** Closes the inline-execution gap where the until-mergeable review drain (v14.32.0) is triggered ONLY by Supervisor Phase 4.5 prompt **step 5.5** — agent-prompt logic, not a hook. On the inline `/autonomous`/`/supervisor` path the Supervisor IS the main thread (not a subagent), so the existing `SubagentStop(supervisor-runner)` hooks never fire either; if the agent skips step 5.5 the drain silently never dispatches (observed: a real `/autonomous` run created a PR and the drain never ran). **(1) Fail-safe hook wrapper (`scripts/hook-dispatch-on-pr-create.sh`, uncounted-sibling-of `dispatch-pr-review.sh` + `test-hook-dispatch-on-pr-create.sh` + a real-payload fixture `scripts/fixtures/posttooluse-gh-pr-create.json`):** a `PostToolUse[Bash]` hook that fires on the actual `gh pr create` Bash tool call (the agent cannot "forget" it), extracts the `https://github.com/.../pull/<n>` URL from `tool_response.stdout`, and **reuses the existing `dispatch-pr-review.sh` as-is** (no new dispatch logic — it owns opt-out resolution, the per-PR idempotency marker, default-ON, the detached launch, and the until-mergeable env-var signal). The fixture envelope was captured from a REAL emitted `PostToolUse[Bash]` payload (never hand-invented — the documented hook-payload-shape footgun). **(2) Session-scope gate (the key safety decision):** dispatch happens ONLY when ALL THREE durable-lifecycle terms hold — (i) `.supervisor/jobs/in-progress/` is non-empty, (ii) `.supervisor/state.md` Status is not `completed`/`failed`, AND (iii) the PR's head branch (current git branch) matches the active session's feature branch from `state.md`. A bare non-empty `in-progress/` is explicitly insufficient. The gate deliberately consumes EXISTING unskippable Phase 1 ACQUIRE writes (branch + `state.md` + the `in-progress/` job move), NOT a new prompt-written marker — a producer marker written by a prompt step would reintroduce the exact step-5.5 prompt-skip fragility this hook exists to fix. **(3) Defense-in-depth, not a replacement:** Supervisor step 5.5 is unchanged; the hook is an additive backstop, and the dispatcher's per-PR idempotency marker guarantees exactly ONE dispatch even if BOTH the hook and step 5.5 fire for the same PR. When both the hook and step 5.5 are live the hook's earlier FINALIZE-time dispatch wins, so the drain runs concurrent with Phase 4.5 self-heal (graceful: the runner never force-pushes and re-polls on a non-fast-forward rejection). **(4) Fail-safe:** any failure mode (missing `jq`/`claude`, malformed payload, no PR URL, gate not satisfied) logs one stderr line and `exit 0` — a `PostToolUse` hook must never break the originating Bash call (mirrors `send-telemetry.sh`/`send-webhook.sh`). A deterministic self-test asserts URL extraction, the AC2 non-PR no-op, the AC3 branch-match gate including the stale-state cases (in-progress empty, Status completed), opt-out delegation, and fail-safe `exit 0` — all 13 cases green, with no regression to `test-dispatch-pr-review.sh` (16 green). **(5) Registration + count:** `hooks/hooks.json` gains exactly ONE `PostToolUse[Bash]` entry; authoritative hook count goes **19 → 20** (`jq '[.hooks[][].hooks[]] | length'`); `check-doc-currency.sh` + `validate-version.sh` green. **(6) Rider fix (surfaced by this PR's own review drain):** also corrects `scripts/dispatch-pr-postmortem.sh` to launch the **namespaced** `/loomwright:pr-postmortem` slash command instead of the bare `/pr-postmortem` — the bare form dies under detached `claude -p` with `Unknown command: /pr-postmortem`, so the churn-gated postmortem that auto-dispatched on PR #67 silently no-op'd and wrote no trend line; `test-dispatch-pr-postmortem.sh` updated to match (16 green). **No new agent / command / skill (counts now 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 20 hooks; the wrapper + test + fixture are uncounted scripts); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.33.0.

**v14.33.0 — `knowledge_sources_used` measurement close-out in `/insights` (Learning Loop Phase 2B):** Closes the measurement half of the v14.28.0 memory APPLY path. Phase 1+2 (v14.28.0) wired memory consults and emitted the optional `knowledge_sources_used` array on `SUPERVISOR_RESULT` / `CODE_REVIEW_RESULT` and the flat `session_end` JSONL line, but `scripts/build-insights.sh` never read it — so the Phase 2 success signal (distinguish *memory existed* from *memory used*) was recorded but not observable. **(1) Aggregation + display:** `build-insights.sh` now reads the flat `session_end.knowledge_sources_used` array and renders a new **`## Knowledge sources (memory APPLY)`** dashboard section — runs-reporting-a-source count, a top-source-tags frequency table, and per-version usage — plus a per-run note line. The section is **suppressed when no run reports a source** (mirroring the System-Twin-hard-signal section; no fabricated zeros) and is tolerant of older logs (absent ⇒ none used). **(2) Self-test:** `scripts/test-insights.sh` gains a knowledge-sources block (present / absent-tolerant / mixed-corpus / suppression); the full suite is 55 assertions, all green. **(3) Docs reconciled:** the stale "build-insights does NOT yet aggregate / deferred follow-up" notes in `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md` and `agents/supervisor.md` now state that aggregation/surfacing landed; `commands/insights.md` documents the new section; `docs/SPIKES/LEARNING_LOOP_ROADMAP.md` marks Phase 2B shipped. **(4) Explicit deferral:** the optional `LAUNCH_PAD_RESULT.knowledge_sources_used` marker is **not** added — `scripts/validate-launch-pad-result.py` enforces a strict four-field, scalar-only discipline (adding an array field is disproportionate and risks parser bugs in a hook validator) and Launch Pad emits no `session_end` line (so the field would not feed `/insights`); the roadmap's Phase 2B AC explicitly permits documenting the Launch Pad gap as deferred. **Advisory / non-gating; no `schema_version` bump; no new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 19 hooks).** Additive on top of v14.32.0.

**v14.32.0 — `/review-pr --until-mergeable` all-channel drain + scoped wait + validate-then-fix + Supervisor auto-run-by-default:** Builds directly on the v14.30.0 `--until-mergeable` heal loop, extending it to drain **all machine-speed external review channels** and wiring it on by default in the Supervisor completion tail — strictly additive, with the never-merge / never-block-on-human / fail-safe-emitter invariants preserved. **(1) Shared bot-review-finding classifier (`scripts/classify-bot-review.sh`, uncounted + `test-classify-bot-review.sh`):** a single source of truth for **bot-authored review-finding** classification on the `(.user.login, .body)` pair (extracted from `pr-postmortem-gather.sh` so the `bot_author_re`/`review_marker_re` patterns live in exactly one place); it works on ANY array of comment-like objects (issue comments, formal review bodies, synthesized check-output objects). It does **NOT** replace the §U3 GraphQL thread bot-vs-human (`__typename`/`login`) classification, which is unchanged. **(2) All-channel read (`review-heal` skill):** each until-mergeable round now computes readiness over the **UNION** of every external PR review channel — formal reviews (`reviews`/`latestReviews`), inline review threads, **PR issue comments** (the #64 channel gap — `gh api .../issues/{n}/comments`, which `gh pr view --json reviews` never returns), and **bot check-run output/annotations** — classifying each channel on its **body/content text** (not metadata) via the shared classifier, plus the **required** check-rollup for the green/settled gate (branch-protection-discovered; only required checks gate, fail-CLOSED to `ESCALATED` on unreadable metadata unless `--required-checks all-non-neutral`). Human approval, `REVIEW_REQUIRED`, and human-authored threads/comments are **surfaced, never drained or gated**. **(3) Scoped wait:** a bounded wait scoped to **required + review-producing checks only** (a configurable name/app pattern via `--review-check-pattern`, default `*review*`/`claude*`; unrelated optional checks are NEVER waited on or escalated by themselves), which re-scans **all** channels once the scoped set settles (so a review comment landing after `ci` went green is seen), and fails CLOSED to `ESCALATED` if a required/review-producing check is still in flight at the bound — all while still NEVER invoking `gh pr merge` and NEVER blocking on a human. **(4) Validate-then-fix:** **replaces** the blind BLOCKING/HIGH severity floor — every detected bot finding (from any channel, at any stated severity) is evidence-validated against the current branch, then fixed at **any** severity if confirmed (the #64 finding was MEDIUM; the old floor would have silently dropped it), surfaced/escalated if confirmed-but-not-auto-fixable, or dismissed if stale/invalid. Confirmed-and-auto-fixable findings are dispatched to the `Task(general-purpose)` fixer (allowlist Read/Write/Edit/Bash/Glob/Grep, no Task), `git push`ed (**never `--force`**), and re-polled. The **READY** definition is refined to "**required checks green AND scoped review-producing checks settled AND no unresolved validated bot findings remain across ALL channels** — human approval/threads surfaced, not gated." **(5) Supervisor auto-run-by-default:** the Supervisor completion tail now auto-runs the until-mergeable drain **by default** via the **existing** fail-safe dispatcher `scripts/dispatch-pr-review.sh` (uncounted, pre-v14.30.0 + extended `test-dispatch-pr-review.sh`; **repurposed** here from the previously opt-in plain-`/review-pr` dispatch — a *new default side-effect*: a `/supervisor` run that produces a PR now launches a detached review-and-heal drain unless opted out — fresh detached `claude -p` process, always exits 0, append-only, can NEVER alter `SUPERVISOR_RESULT.status`/`heal_decision`), opt-out via `--no-until-mergeable` / `auto_until_mergeable: false` (`.supervisor/notify-config.json`) / `LOOMWRIGHT_UNTIL_MERGEABLE`. **(6) Schema + consumers:** additive `REVIEW_HEAL_RESULT` fields (stays **`schema_version: 2`**; v1 still accepted) + additive `SUPERVISOR_RESULT` fields (stays **`schema_version: 1`**) documented in `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md`; the `/autonomous` consumer docs continue to treat unrecognized/`READY` as a terminal non-re-iterate state. Default `/review-pr` (no `--until-mergeable`) remains byte-for-byte the v14.30.0-and-earlier diff-only review→fix→re-review loop. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 19 hooks — `classify-bot-review.sh` + `dispatch-pr-review.sh` are uncounted scripts and `/review-pr` already exists); no `schema_version` bump.** Additive on top of v14.31.0.

**v14.31.0 — Generic Launch Pad + Plan Reviewer prevention guardrails:** Three additive, repo-agnostic, **trigger-gated** prevention guardrails that catch a class of planning gaps before they surface as review churn — with no behavior change on ordinary briefs. **(1) Validator-owned-surfaces (Launch Pad Phase 3 ANALYZE):** when a task's acceptance depends on a generated/mechanical validation script (doc-currency / lint / codegen / consistency checker — stated generically, **never a hard-coded filename**), Launch Pad folds the surfaces that validator scans into the File Impact Map (or explicitly notes they may need updates), so a brief can no longer omit files a mechanical gate will later flag. **(2) Dirty-source-doc warning (Launch Pad Phase 1 VALIDATE):** when the specific source requirement/doc a brief is planned from is uncommitted in the working tree, Launch Pad emits a WARNING that the brief's provenance/staleness is unverified — scoped to that one source file, never a whole-tree dirty check. **(3) Plan Reviewer Criterion 15 (Canonical-List / Source-of-Truth Verification):** a conditional criterion that fires only when a brief asserts a canonical-source claim ("single source of truth" / "canonical list" / "exact names" / "authoritative") **and** cites a source — it then verifies the brief's restated values against that cited source and flags mismatches under a new `canonical_source` PLAN_REVIEW_RESULT issue category; absent that trigger it skips silently (the Criteria 11/13/14 conditional precedent). Note the **gating asymmetry**: guardrails (1)+(2) are advisory-only (warn / annotate, never block), whereas Criterion 15 — like Plan Reviewer's other numbered criteria — is **gating**, so a verified gate-breaking mismatch on a *triggering* brief can reach BLOCKING→FAIL and block the save (the "no behavior change" above refers to *non-triggering* briefs). `canonical_source` is an **additive free-form** issue category (the `executable_acceptance` precedent) — **no `schema_version` bump** (PLAN_REVIEW_RESULT stays `schema_version: 1`). **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 19 hooks).** Additive on top of v14.30.0.

**v14.30.0 — `/review-pr --until-mergeable` external-review-aware heal loop + churn-gated auto-postmortem:** Closes the long-standing gap that the entire review-and-heal machinery (Supervisor Phase 4.5, standalone `/review-pr` + `review-heal`, `/autonomous` EVALUATE) was a **closed internal loop** — it ran *our own* `code-reviewer` against the integrated diff and exited, never consulting external GitHub review state (`statusCheckRollup`, `reviews`, `reviewDecision`, review threads) and never re-acting on what the PR's reviewers/bots flagged. This adds an **opt-in, default-OFF** `--until-mergeable` mode to `/review-pr` + the `review-heal` skill that drains the *machine-speed* external signals and loops until the PR is mechanically merge-ready, **without ever merging and without ever waiting on a human**. **(1) External-state drain (AC1–AC3, AC14, AC15):** each round reads `gh pr view <url> --json statusCheckRollup,reviews,latestReviews,reviewDecision,mergeable,mergeStateStatus`, PLUS `gh api graphql` for review threads (`reviewThreads { isResolved, comments(first:1){ nodes{ author{ login __typename } } } }` — the thread state + author type that `gh pr view --json` does not expose), PLUS branch-protection **required-check discovery** (`gh api .../branches/{base}/protection/required_status_checks` or GraphQL `requiredStatusCheckContexts`). It auto-fixes the actionable BLOCKING/HIGH items via a `Task(general-purpose)` worker (allowlist Read/Write/Edit/Bash/Glob/Grep, **no Task**), `git push`es (**never `--force`**), and re-polls. **(2) Readiness semantics:** READY ⇔ **all required checks green AND no unresolved bot-authored review threads remain**. Required checks are gated from branch-protection metadata only (optional/non-required failing checks never block); when that metadata is unreadable the loop **fails CLOSED to `ESCALATED`** unless `--required-checks all-non-neutral` opts in. Threads count as READY-blockers only when the first comment's author is a bot (`author.__typename == "Bot"` or login `*[bot]`); **human approval, `REVIEW_REQUIRED`, and human-authored threads are surfaced but NEVER awaited**. On READY the loop stops and fires a best-effort desktop/webhook "ready to merge" notification. **(3) Bounds & anti-churn (AC4–AC6):** bounded by `--max-rounds N` (default 5) → `ESCALATED` + `gh pr comment` of remaining findings; an anti-churn fingerprint guardrail (`{file, issue_category, rule}`, trip on repeat-after-fix or `churn_rounds ≥ 2`) runs **one** deep "fix-the-class" self-review per trip; **no `gh pr merge` is ever invoked** in any terminal state (never-auto-merge invariant preserved). **(4) Churn-gated auto-postmortem (AC9–AC13):** on loop exit a new fail-safe dispatcher `scripts/dispatch-pr-postmortem.sh` (uncounted; mirrors `dispatch-pr-review.sh`) conditionally fires the existing read-only `/pr-postmortem <pr-url>` as a fresh detached `claude` process — **churn-gated** (`postmortem_churn_threshold`, default 2, via `--postmortem-churn-threshold` or `.supervisor/notify-config.json`; no-op on clean low-churn PRs), opt-out via `--no-auto-postmortem` / `auto_postmortem: false`. The `REVIEW_HEAL_RESULT.decision` is computed and emitted **before** dispatch and the dispatcher always exits 0 / only appends to `.supervisor/postmortem/results.jsonl`, so postmortem can **NEVER** alter the merge-ready result (the cardinal fail-safe guarantee). **(5) Schema + consumers (AC8, AC16):** `REVIEW_HEAL_RESULT` goes to **`schema_version: 2`** (adds `READY` decision + new fields + the GraphQL/required-check note in `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md`; v1 still accepted), and the `/autonomous` consumer docs (`commands/autonomous.md`, `skills/autonomous-loop/SKILL.md`) list `READY` as additive, noting EVALUATE never emits it (it does not pass `--until-mergeable`) and treats unrecognized/`READY` as a terminal non-re-iterate state (degrades safely). **(6) Strictly additive (AC7):** `/review-pr` without `--until-mergeable` is byte-for-byte the existing diff-only review→fix→re-review loop. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 19 hooks — `dispatch-pr-postmortem.sh` is an uncounted script and `/pr-postmortem` already exists); no `SUPERVISOR_RESULT` schema_version bump.** Additive on top of v14.29.0.

**v14.29.0 — Learning Loop Phase 3 residual: CI miss-class vocabulary + drift-taxonomy split + opt-in advisory red-team:** Ships the residual of Learning Loop Phase 3 (its core landed in v14.21.0 as the self-heal miss-class hardening); this release closes the three remaining refinements as **additive, advisory** review-quality levers with **no `schema_version` bump and counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 19 hooks**. **(R4) CI miss-class vocabulary** — the independent CI review prompt (`.github/workflows/claude-code-review.yml`) gains the six named behavioral/doc miss-classes from the Self-Heal Miss-Class Checklist (`validation parity`, `numeric falsy coercion`, `positional args vs options object misuse`, `missing branch coverage`, `count/version/restated-list drift`, `cross-reference precision drift`) as the CI reviewer's own checklist — so the local self-heal review and the independent CI review share one defect vocabulary, while CI stays explicitly **independent** (the prompt directs it not to converge with any other reviewer). **(R3) Miss-class taxonomy split** — the previously merged "count / cross-reference drift" entry in the Self-Heal Miss-Class Checklist (`skills/quality-checklist/SKILL.md`) is split into two distinct named classes — **count/version/restated-list drift** (stale counts, version annotations, re-stated lists that diverge from the authoritative source) and **cross-reference precision drift** (a `see X` / `file:line` / anchor pointer that no longer resolves to what it claims) — and the self-heal lens enumerations in `agents/code-reviewer.md`, `agents/supervisor.md`, and `skills/pr-postmortem/SKILL.md` are reconciled to the six canonical names. This is reviewer-guidance prose only — the orthogonal `CODE_REVIEW_RESULT.drift_kind` enum is **unchanged**. **(R1) Opt-in advisory red-team lens** — Supervisor Phase 4.5 gains an **opt-in, default-OFF, fail-safe, NON-gating** advisory red-team pass (`--red-team` / `--no-red-team` flags, `.red_team_high_risk` config) that runs only on high-risk integrated diffs and records its findings in `SUPERVISOR_RESULT.summary` + the job Outcome block. It can NEVER block a PR, change a `heal_decision`, or fail the run — exactly the advisory contract of the existing Phase 4.5 hard-signal lenses; absent the flag/config the gate behaves byte-identically to v14.28.0. **No schema change** (red-team findings ride in the existing `summary` + Outcome surfaces — RESULT_SCHEMAS.md untouched). **No new agent / command / skill / hook; counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 19 hooks.** Additive on top of v14.28.0.

**v14.28.0 — Memory APPLY path + `knowledge_sources_used` usage telemetry (Learning Loop Phase 1+2):** Closes the APPLY half of the learning loop the memory consume-path (v14.5.0) deferred: the Agent Manager already *writes* memory (verified `LESSONS.md` via `/dreaming`, `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`, per-agent memory) but applied it inconsistently — Code Reviewer never explicitly consulted memory, Launch Pad / Supervisor read project memory but not verified lessons, and no run could report which knowledge it used. This wires every memory-bearing read path into an **explicit, advisory, fail-safe, NON-gating APPLY step** and adds one additive optional `knowledge_sources_used` telemetry marker so usage becomes measurable — with **zero** new storage surface and **zero** gate/verdict changes. **(1) Code Reviewer** (`agents/code-reviewer.md`) gains a bounded read-only "Consult memory (advisory, read-only)" step at Context Setup that uses its preloaded per-agent memory (resolved read-only via a `.claude/agent-memory/*code-reviewer*` glob when an explicit filesystem read is needed) plus `read-project-memory.sh`, and records what it used via the additive `knowledge_sources_used` field on `CODE_REVIEW_RESULT` (stays `schema_version 3`). **(2) Launch Pad** (`agents/launch-pad.md`) gains a "Consult verified lessons (advisory)" step adjacent to its existing project-memory consult (Phase 3 ANALYZE) that runs `read-lessons.sh`; considered verified lessons may be **cited** in the brief's Risk Assessment / analysis. **(3) Supervisor** (`agents/supervisor.md`) extends its Phase 1 ACQUIRE project-memory consult with a sibling `read-lessons.sh` consult, and emits the additive `knowledge_sources_used` array **both** nested on `SUPERVISOR_RESULT` (stays `schema_version 1`) **and** as a flat field on the `session_end` JSONL line (the surface `build-insights.sh` / `/insights` reads — same dual-shape pattern as `contract_conformance`; wiring `build-insights.sh` to aggregate/display it is an intentional later follow-up). **Framing on every new read:** advisory and strictly subordinate to `CLAUDE.md` (on conflict CLAUDE.md wins); the reader is fail-safe (always exits 0, emits only provenance-verified non-stale entries; absent/empty ⇒ proceed normally); reading memory MUST NEVER block the run or change a verdict / `heal_decision`. **(4) Docs:** `AGENT_GUIDELINES.md` documents the memory hierarchy & APPLY precedence (`CLAUDE.md` > `PROJECT_MEMORY` > `LESSONS` > per-agent memory > brain/wiki hints — layers 2–5 advisory, never override CLAUDE.md or any gate); `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md` documents the optional, additive, non-gating `knowledge_sources_used` array on SUPERVISOR_RESULT / CODE_REVIEW_RESULT and the `session_end` JSONL (absent ⇒ valid; not enumerated by the SubagentStop hook; **no `schema_version` bump** — the `branch_base` / `pr_state` / `preflight_sync` additive precedent). The field is a JSON array of lowercase source tags (`project_memory`, `lessons:<category>`, `agent_memory:<agent>`, `twin:<path>`, `brain_context`). **Non-Goals (explicitly not done):** no `/setup brain`, no brain write-back, no trusted-wiki writes, no worker memory reads, no gating/verdict/`heal_decision` changes, no new memory directories, no vector/RAG, no `schema_version` bumps. **No new agent / command / skill / hook; counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 19 hooks.** Additive on top of v14.27.0.

**v14.27.0 — Brain-context read-on-demand skill + baseline eval harness (brain-integration Phase 0+1):** Lands the first two phases of the brain-integration design doc (`docs/SPIKES/BRAIN_INTEGRATION_EVOLUTION.md`) — the personal-brain-as-System-Twin-substrate direction — as a small, **advisory, read-only, and fail-safe** in-repo capability. **(1) New `brain-context` skill** (`skills/brain-context/`): gives Launch Pad, Code Reviewer, and the Supervisor an **on-demand in-repo read path** into accumulated project knowledge, consulted via a deliberate Read at decision points — **deliberately NOT preloaded** into any agent's frontmatter, so it adds zero spawn-time tokens to those agents and is paid for only when an agent actually reaches for it. The read path is strictly **advisory**: it never gates a PR, never changes a `heal_decision`, never blocks or mutates anything, and any read miss / missing source / parse failure degrades to a **logged no-op** (per the CLAUDE.md bimodal "side-effect emitters fail SAFE" invariant). **(2) Baseline eval harness** for measuring read-path usefulness over time — the seed instrument for evaluating whether brain-context reads actually improve agent decisions, distinct from the existing eval / ground-truth / benchmark runners. **Counts move 14 / 18 / 54 / 19 → 14 agents / 18 commands / 55 skills / 19 hooks — +1 skill (`brain-context`); no new agent, command, or hook; no schema_version bumps.** Additive on top of v14.26.0.

**v14.26.0 — Close the requirement→brief→done loop (Beads-optional):** Closes a gap in the Beads-absent flow (Product Owner → `.supervisor/requirements/*.md` → Launch Pad → Supervisor): until now a Product Owner requirement file was write-once with no close-out marker, so done and not-done requirements looked identical on disk and operators had to hand-correlate slugs between `requirements/` and `jobs/done/`. This wires a provenance link end-to-end so a requirement that produced a successfully-completed brief is marked done automatically, with Beads remaining the sole source of truth when it is active. **Ownership split:** Launch Pad (the only agent that knows requirement→brief) owns the *provenance link*; the Supervisor completion tail (the only thing alive at done-time, already the sole writer of job lifecycle) owns the *close-out*. **(1) Producer** — `agents/launch-pad.md` Phase 2 step 0 records the resolved `.supervisor/requirements/*.md` path as `source_requirement` (only for requirement-file inputs; unset for literal-string goals or repo files outside the handoff dir), and Phase 5 PACKAGE emits a `- **Source requirement:** {repo-root-relative path}` line under the brief's `## Environment` only when resolved (omitted entirely otherwise — no placeholder); the optional field is documented in the `skills/supervisor-readiness/SKILL.md` brief template. **(2) Consumer** — `agents/supervisor.md` Phase 4.5 SELF_HEAL completion-tail gains step 2.5: on a successful outcome (PASS / loop-skipped / ESCALATED) **and only when Beads is inactive** (`test -d .beads && bd --version`), it reads the provenance pointer, path-safety-checks it (must resolve **under `.supervisor/requirements/`** AND pass `test -f` — guards traversal/injection via the brief field), and stamps an **idempotent** `## Status` block (`Status`, `Completed`, `Brief`, `PR`) on the originating requirement file **in place** (stamp-not-move handles the multi-brief case; replace-not-duplicate so the latest close-out wins). The `Status` value **mirrors the brief `## Outcome` granularity** — PASS / loop-skipped → `done`; ESCALATED → `done_with_escalation` plus a `- **Heal:**` reason + remaining-issue line — so an escalated requirement is not indistinguishable from a clean pass. The whole step is a **fail-safe runtime side-effect emitter**: any error (unreadable brief, missing file, write failure, malformed pointer, path outside the handoff dir) is a logged no-op that NEVER propagates to `SUPERVISOR_RESULT.status` and never fails the run (per the CLAUDE.md bimodal "side-effect emitters fail SAFE" invariant); a `failed` / abort / checkpoint / base-mismatch / invariant-violation run never reaches it, so a failed run never marks a requirement done. **(3) Docs** — `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md` documents both the brief `Source requirement` field and the requirement `## Status` close-out convention (advisory / Beads-absent-only, mirrors the brief `## Outcome` pattern). **Prompt/doc-only; no new agent / command / skill / hook; no schema_version bumps. Counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 54 skills / 19 hooks.** Additive on top of v14.25.1.

**v14.25.1 — Codify illustrative example values as version-agnostic (patch):** A PR-review exchange surfaced a recurring trap: stale-looking `plugin_version` values inside *illustrative* example blocks (the sample `session_end` / `POSTMORTEM_RESULT` JSONL records in `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md`, the mirror `session_end` block + `e.g. "X.Y.Z"` prose placeholders in `agents/supervisor.md`) read as version drift, but they have **no currency requirement** — the real value is read at runtime from `plugin.json` via jq, and `scripts/check-doc-currency.sh` deliberately does not scan them. Bumping them every release is a drift-treadmill the gate cannot enforce (they just re-stale at the next version). Rather than chase them, this release **codifies the convention**: illustrative example values are version-agnostic and deliberately frozen — documented in CLAUDE.md §"Doc currency is CI-enforced" and beside the `plugin_version` field spec in `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md`, so future reviews don't re-flag them. Genuine current-claims (manifest `version`/headline, `plugin.json (vX.Y.Z)` annotations, the CLAUDE.md banner) continue to track the live version under the gate, and are advanced here to 14.25.1. **No example values are bumped. Documentation-only; no agent / skill / hook behavior changes; no schema_version bumps. Counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 54 skills / 19 hooks.** Additive patch on top of v14.25.0.

**v14.25.0 — Auto-author Outcomes Rubric in `/autonomous` multi-iteration mode:** The `/autonomous` loop becomes a rubric **producer**, not just a consumer. Until now the loop's rubric gate could only *consume* an `## Outcomes Rubric` a human had already written into the requirement; runs whose requirements lacked one silently fell through to the no-rubric gate every iteration. This release teaches the inlined Launch Pad to **auto-author** a rubric when one is absent in multi-iteration mode: at a guarded Phase 5 step, Launch Pad distills 3–7 **diff-checkable** outcome bullets from the Acceptance Criteria plus its Phase 3 file-impact analysis, the human approves or edits them at the existing Launch Pad Phase 6 human gate (no new gate, no fire-and-forget authoring), and the `/autonomous` loop then **persists the approved rubric back into the requirement body and freezes it** so every later iteration scores against the same stable rubric. **Degenerate fallback:** if fewer than 3 genuinely diff-checkable bullets can be produced, no rubric is authored and the pre-existing no-rubric gate applies exactly as before — the producer never manufactures a weak rubric to satisfy the gate. This is a **documentation/prompt-only** change: `skills/supervisor-readiness/SKILL.md` gains a new §"Auto-Authoring (multi-iteration)" (version 1.1.0→1.1.1); `agents/launch-pad.md` gains the guarded Phase 5 authoring step (gated on multi-iteration + rubric-absent); `skills/autonomous-loop/SKILL.md` extends PLAN steps 2 & 7 to request authoring and persist+freeze the result (version 1.3.0→1.3.1); `agents/plan-reviewer.md` extends Criterion 3 to validate an auto-authored rubric (still 14 criteria — no new criterion); `commands/autonomous.md` and `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md` document the behavior. **No new agent / command / skill / hook; no schema_version bumps. Counts unchanged at 14 agents / 18 commands / 54 skills / 19 hooks.** Additive on top of v14.24.0.

**v14.24.0 — `/setup` umbrella command + observability module:** New `/setup` slash command — a status dashboard plus guided configuration for every optional plugin capability across 6 modules (observability, telemetry, notifications, webhook, Beads, MySQL MCP; `/setup telemetry` delegates to the unchanged `/telemetry`), governed by the new `setup` skill (check → report → offer → apply → verify, idempotent, never blind-overwrite). The observability module ships a bundled **local Langfuse v3 + OTel collector stack** under `scripts/otel/` (every image pinned — Langfuse **3.82.0**, `otel-collector-contrib` **0.116.1**): assets are copied at init time to `~/.claude/loomwright/observability/`, and the 8-key env block (`CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TELEMETRY=1`, the three `OTEL_*_EXPORTER=otlp` keys, `http/protobuf` protocol, endpoint, headers, `OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES=service.version=<plugin version>`) is jq-deep-merged into user-scope `~/.claude/settings.json` with a timestamped backup and abort-on-parse-failure. Emission is **native-OTel-only** — the plugin emits no spans itself; the collector renames Claude-Code token attributes to `gen_ai.usage.*` and forwards TRACES ONLY to Langfuse's `/api/public/otel` (documented Langfuse limitation — metrics/logs terminate in a debug exporter). A **session-start health probe** extends `session-resume.sh` (env-block-gated, 1s curl, 24h debounce marker, never starts Docker, prints the exact restart command) — `hooks.json` untouched, hook count stays 19. Additive **`plugin_version` stamping** flows through supervisor `session_end`, the `POSTMORTEM_RESULT` trend line (pr-postmortem skill → 1.3.0), the telemetry payload, and a per-version `build-insights` table. New reference doc `docs/OBSERVABILITY.md` (architecture, env-block reference, pinned versions, troubleshooting incl. the collector's distroless non-root UID 10001 first-boot volume-permission gotcha). **No new hooks; no schema_version bumps. Counts are now 14 agents / 18 commands / 54 skills / 19 hooks — +1 command (`/setup`) +1 skill (`setup`).** Additive on top of v14.23.3.

**v14.23.3 — `/pr-postmortem` "address code review" adjacency fix (patch):** Closes the third small `is_review_fix` shape gap: the first regex alternative `address(es)? review` required ADJACENCY, so explicit headlines like `fix(reports): address code review findings on favorite-reports feature` / `fix(reporting): address code review findings #3 #5 #7 #10` never matched — the intervening word "code" broke them (verified live on a private downstream repo, 2026-06-12: counted `review_rounds: 1` via formal_reviews while 2 explicit review-fix commits went uncounted, true ≈3; the trend line documents the gap). One minimal anchored widening in `scripts/pr-postmortem-gather.sh`: the alternative becomes `address(es)? (code )?review` (case-insensitive), in the same spirit as v14.23.2's explicit forms — NOT a re-broadening to the rejected churn-word class: the literal word "review" stays mandatory, and `nit` / unanchored `round-N` / `findings` stay rejected and test-pinned ("add audit findings export" is the in-fixture negative control). Self-test grows to 15 cases (OTHERSVC #139-shape fixture: both "address code review findings" headlines flag `is_review_fix`; rounds 1 → 2 via fix_commits); `pr-postmortem` skill → 1.2.1. **POSTMORTEM_RESULT unchanged at `schema_version: 1`; no new agent / command / skill / hook (still 14 / 17 / 53 / 19).** Additive patch on top of v14.23.2.

**v14.23.2 — `/pr-postmortem` bot-comment-shape review-round detection (patch):** Closes the residual `review_rounds: 0` undercount that BOTH v14.23.1 signals still missed on the bot-comment review shape (verified live on a private downstream repo, 2026-06-10: 3 explicit review-fix rounds, counted 0; trend line documents the gap). Two targeted widenings in `scripts/pr-postmortem-gather.sh`; the v14.23.1 architecture is unchanged (three-signal MAX, `review_rounds_source`, per_page-bounded fail-safe comments fetch, `strings`-guarded untrusted fields). **(1) `is_review_fix` gains two EXPLICIT anchored forms** — `pr #[0-9]+ review` and `review #[0-9]+` (case-insensitive, word-bounded so "preview #2" can never match) — covering headlines like `fix(X): PR #146 review — …` / `fix(X): review #2 — …`. This is NOT a re-broadening to the rejected churn-word class: `nit` / unanchored `round-N` / `findings` stay rejected and test-pinned; the new forms carry the literal word "review" adjacent to a PR/round number. **(2) The bot-comment review marker widens** from heading-anchored ("a markdown heading containing review", or "code review") to a word-bounded `review` ANYWHERE in the bot comment body — that shape's `claude[bot]` comments open with `## Overview` and mention review only in running text, so the old marker matched none of them. False-round control still rests on the unchanged author gate + at-least-one-commit-after timestamp anchor, and the "Deploy Preview" non-match guarantee stays test-pinned (word boundary). Self-test grows to 14 cases (bot-comment-shape fixture: 0 review objects, `## Overview`-headed bot comments, explicit fix headlines — both signals independently count 3); `pr-postmortem` skill → 1.2.0. **POSTMORTEM_RESULT unchanged at `schema_version: 1`; no new agent / command / skill / hook (still 14 / 17 / 53 / 19).** Additive patch on top of v14.23.1.

**v14.23.1 — Residual-defect sweep + `review_rounds` bot-comment signal (patch):** Two strands of post-merge defect repair; no new counted artifacts. **(1) `/pr-postmortem` review-rounds undercount fixed:** repos whose review feedback arrives as CI-workflow ISSUE comments (e.g. `claude[bot]` from a claude-review workflow) — not GitHub review objects — read `review_rounds: 0` despite real churn (verified live: PRs #47/#48 each had 0 review objects but 7 `claude[bot]` comments). `scripts/pr-postmortem-gather.sh` now makes a second fail-safe, read-only `gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/issues/{n}/comments?per_page=100` fetch (bounded single page; `--paginate` would break the single-value `--argjson` parse) and derives `review_rounds` as the MAX of **three** signals: review-fix commit headlines, formal churn-review submissions, and **bot-authored issue-comment review rounds** — a comment whose author looks like a review bot (login `claude`, `github-actions*`, or any `*[bot]`) and whose body carries a review marker (a markdown heading containing the word "review", or "code review", word-bounded so "Deploy Preview" never matches), counted as a round only when at least one commit lands after it (comment `created_at` vs commit `committedDate`). A `review_rounds_source` note (`fix_commits` | `formal_reviews` | `bot_comments` | `none`) lives in the gather output only — **POSTMORTEM_RESULT is unchanged at `schema_version: 1`**. The `is_review_fix` headline regex deliberately stays narrow: broadened churn-word matching (`nit` / `round-N` / `findings`) was evaluated and rejected as false-positive-prone; the timestamp-anchored bot-round signal carries the counting instead (test-pinned). The comments fetch degrades to the two legacy signals on ANY failure (never an unavailable emit, always exit 0), and untrusted comment fields are `strings`-guarded inside the single jq invocation so a hostile-typed element (non-string `user.login` / `body` / `created_at`) degrades element-locally instead of aborting the emit; the self-test grows to 13 cases (CI-comment churn, `gh api`-failure degrade, hostile-typed comment elements); `pr-postmortem` skill → 1.1.0. **(2) Post-merge residual-defect sweep (~10 contract/doc defects):** supervisor tool-budget thresholds corrected to the 50-call budget zones (40+/80% checkpoint, 46+/92% exit); QA_RESULT `status` enum gains session-mode `plan_created` / `all_scopes_completed` (schema + qa-executor hook + a new contract-parity ENUMS row); execute-manager adjudication EXECUTE_CHECKPOINT templates carry the hook-required base fields; context-keeper atomicity wording matches its real toolset (no Bash → no temp-file rename); launch-pad example `requires` entries gain the mandatory `from:` field; brainstorming forced-concession wording aligned to the honest-outcome rule; `.supervisor/jobs/` → `.supervisor/jobs/pending/` sweep + fictional token-savings numbers deleted; GATE_VERDICT gate numbers typed `number` (fractional gates 0.5/1.5 are valid); code-reviewer drift-FAIL wording + environment-blocked NEEDS_HUMAN row; worker summary-file contract accepts the documented `summary_file_write_failed` degradation marker. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (still 14 / 17 / 53 / 19); all result schemas unchanged.** Additive patch on top of v14.23.0.

**v14.23.0 — Supervisor diet + contract-parity CI guard:** Two structural quality levers. **(1) `self-heal-advisory` skill:** the advisory-only System Twin machinery of Supervisor Phase 4.5 — the contract-conformance READ, benchmark run, ground-truth execution, the contract-builder WRITE-path spawn (incident maps included), the advisory Twin delta line, and the hard-signal dual-emission table — moves out of `agents/supervisor.md` (~220 lines) into the new `skills/self-heal-advisory/SKILL.md`. The agent prompt keeps the load-bearing gates (review-and-fix loop, completion-tail guard, job lifecycle, SUPERVISOR_RESULT emission, rubric-grader spawn) plus short invariant-preserving pointers, and reads the skill **on demand at Phase 4.5 entry** (deliberately NOT preloaded — preloading would re-inject the extracted lines at spawn). The skill covers ONLY Supervisor-specific advisory extras; review→fix→re-review loop semantics remain owned by `skills/review-heal/SKILL.md`. Advisory contract unchanged: nothing in the skill ever changes `heal_decision` or blocks the PR. **(2) `scripts/check-contract-parity.sh`:** a fourth CI guard (wired into `ci.yml` after check-doc-currency) that mechanically checks hook↔prompt contract parity: for each prompt-type SubagentStop validator in `hooks.json` it verifies the hook-required field names appear in the matched agent's emit-format section, plus an enum/literal rule for result `status`/`decision` fields (flags literals outside the validator's enum — the class that caught `status: paused`). Self-test at `loomwright/scripts/test-check-contract-parity.sh` (auto-run by CI's `test-*.sh` convention). This guard would have mechanically caught the v14.22.x hook-rejection traps (qa-executor missing `schema_version`/`summary`, execute-manager stale formats, the illegal `paused` status). **Current counts are now 14 / 17 / 53 / 19 — +1 skill (`self-heal-advisory`); no new agent, command, or hook.** Additive on top of v14.22.0.

**v14.22.0 — `/pr-postmortem`: on-demand PR review-churn root-cause analysis:** A new read-only, advisory-only diagnostic that turns post-PR review churn into a measurable trend. After an agent-generated PR has absorbed multiple rounds of review-and-fix, `/pr-postmortem <pr-url>` gathers the PR's metadata + review threads + diff stats via the new `scripts/pr-postmortem-gather.sh`, buckets each review round into one of six reproducible root-cause classes (`plan_gap`, `missing_context`, `convention_mismatch`, `execution_bug`, `quality_gap`, `scope_too_large`) — plus an optional `self_heal_miss` flag for rounds the Phase 4.5 self-heal should have caught — and attributes each to the agent-flow stage that should have caught it (`launch_pad` / `worker` / `self_heal` / `unknowable`), prints a human-readable root-cause report, and appends **exactly one** jq-built, fail-safe `POSTMORTEM_RESULT` trend line to `.supervisor/postmortem/results.jsonl`. It **never writes code, never gates, never blocks the PR** — the append is best-effort (any `jq`/IO failure prints one warning and still exits 0; the report is already printed). The accumulated trend file is the **seed corpus for the deferred M2b part-2b synthetic eval harness**. Ships as: the `pr-postmortem` skill (the analysis protocol + miss-class taxonomy, authority for the command), the `commands/pr-postmortem.md` entry point, the read-only `scripts/pr-postmortem-gather.sh` gather (+ its self-test), a `POSTMORTEM_RESULT` schema section in `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md` (`schema_version: 1`, **no hook validator** — the command is the main agent of its own session), and a `SKILLS_INDEX.md` row. **Current counts are now 14 agents / 17 commands / 52 skills / 19 hooks — +1 command +1 skill (the `pr-postmortem` analyzer); no new agent, no new hook.** Additive on top of v14.21.0.

**v14.21.0 — Self-heal hardening: make Phase 4.5 catch the post-PR review blind spot:** Across recent PRs, 8/8 `session_end` records reported `heal_decision: PASS` (5 with `heal_iterations: 0` — heal found nothing) yet those PRs still absorbed 3–6 rounds of post-PR review-and-fix. Root cause: Phase 4.5 re-ran the SAME diff-scoped Code Reviewer (inheriting its per-subtask blind spots) and `ground_truth` was `skipped` everywhere. Four **advisory-only, additive** levers fix this. **(1) Different-lens review:** for non-stacked (`main`-based) heal runs, the Phase 4.5 `code-reviewer` spawn now directs `consistency_audit` mode (not a plain `diff_review`) when self-repo trigger paths match, and ALWAYS applies the new repo-agnostic miss-class checklist — a *different lens* than the per-subtask reviewer whose blind spots it would otherwise inherit; the stacked-iteration DIFF-SCOPE OVERRIDE (non-`main` bases) is left intact. **(2) Repo-agnostic Self-Heal Miss-Class Checklist:** a new section in `skills/quality-checklist/SKILL.md` (pointer in `agents/code-reviewer.md`) covering backend-mirrors-frontend validation parity, no `||`/falsy coercion on numeric fields, no positional args to options-object functions, missing branch coverage, and count/cross-ref drift — repo-agnostic so it catches these classes on external app repos where the plugin's `consistency_audit` triggers never fire. **(3) Fix-the-class, not the instance:** the Phase 4.5 fixer now sweeps the full feature-branch diff for every other occurrence of a flagged finding's class and fixes them all — the reviewer samples, the fixer sweeps. **(4) `ground_truth` actually runs for plugin-self doc-surface briefs:** Launch Pad Phase 5 now affirmatively emits `## Executable Acceptance` with `corpus-task: doc-currency-green` + `version-consistent` for plugin-self/doc-surface briefs (authoring convention mirrored in `supervisor-readiness/SKILL.md`), so Phase 4.5 `ground_truth` runs the doc/version invariants (`status != skipped`) instead of finding nothing to execute. **Advisory contract preserved:** `heal_decision` semantics unchanged, the PR is never blocked, `SUPERVISOR_RESULT` stays `schema_version: 1`. The roadmap records the hardening and the deferred M2b part-2b token-tracking constraint (the future synthetic/headless-`claude` eval harness defaults to `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`, needs no paid API credits, and must report per-run token usage). **No new agent / command / hook / skill (still 14 / 16 / 51 / 19) — the miss-class checklist is a section in an existing skill, not a new skill.** Additive on top of v14.20.0.

**v14.20.0 — System Twin M2b part-2a: wire the fitness instruments + self-test suite into CI (advisory report + hard gates):** Makes Pillar 2's "provable-done" instruments **continuous in CI** — additive, advisory-first, no counted artifacts. **(1) Full self-test suite as a HARD gate:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` now runs the deterministic self-test suite (`loomwright/scripts/test-*.sh`) via a loop (echoing `== <test> ==`, fail-fast on first non-zero) instead of just `test-run-eval.sh`, so **all 14** deterministic self-tests gate every push/PR to `main` and future `test-*.sh` are auto-included (anti-drift); only `test-build-vault.sh`'s platform-dependent idempotency assertion (#3) is skipped in CI (via `BUILD_VAULT_SKIP_IDEMPOTENCY` — it fails on ubuntu/ext4 but passes on macOS, while that file's other assertions, including the path-escape / write-containment safety checks, keep gating; pre-existing `build-vault.sh` / `/obsidian` bug tracked in issue #40); the three existing version/doc gates (`validate-version.sh`, `check-command-sync.sh`, `check-doc-currency.sh`) still hard-gate. **(2) Advisory fitness report (NON-gating):** a new uncounted helper `scripts/ci-fitness-report.sh` runs the three fitness runners (`run-eval.sh`, `run-ground-truth.sh --check 'corpus-task: version-consistent'`, `run-benchmark.sh`), tolerantly parses each one-line JSON result with `jq`, and writes a markdown summary (eval `pass_rate`+`status`, ground-truth `status`+`checks_passed/total`, benchmark `metric`+`value`+`status`) to `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY`. The step is wrapped (`continue-on-error: true` + `|| true` atop the runners' always-exit-0 contract + tolerant `jq` parsing) so a low/`unverified`/`skipped` fitness result can NEVER red the build — only the self-tests + version/doc gates can. **(3) Fitness-history artifact:** `.supervisor/eval/results.jsonl` is uploaded best-effort (`if-no-files-found: ignore`). **(4) Docs:** the eval-corpus `README.md` and `SYSTEM_TWIN_ROADMAP.md` now record part-2a shipped (advisory CI fitness instruments + self-test hard gate) with **part-2b** (headless `claude` generation / the full agent loop in CI — needs `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, a token budget, a circuit-breaker) and **M3** (advisory→gating flip) still deferred. `claude-code-review.yml` / `claude.yml` untouched; **NO new secrets**. **No new agent / command / hook / skill (still 14 / 16 / 51 / 19); 1 new uncounted helper script.** Additive on top of v14.19.0.

**v14.19.0 — System Twin M2b slice 1a: an advisory ground-truth execution step in Supervisor Phase 4.5:** Lands the **ground-truth execution muscle** slice of milestone M2b (Pillar 2, "provable-done") — **advisory, additive, and with zero counted artifacts.** **(1) `scripts/run-ground-truth.sh`** — a deterministic, fail-safe runner that resolves a project's **declared executable-acceptance checks** and runs them, reporting a hard pass/fail on real behavior. The declaration source is a brief's `## Executable Acceptance` section (when present) or, as a fallback, a `.supervisor/twin/ground-truth.json` file; each declared check carries a `kind`: **`cmd:`** (an arbitrary shell command — exit 0 = pass, non-0 = fail), **`corpus-task:`** (run a single `eval-corpus/<task-id>/check.sh` task), or **`qa-executor:`** (recognized but **DEFERRED to slice 1b** — recorded as a per-check `unverified` with reason `qa_executor_dispatch_deferred_m2b_1b`, never run in this slice). The script prints a human/grep per-check block + a `Checks passed: M/N` line and exactly ONE machine-readable `GROUND_TRUTH_JSON: {...}` line (jq-built for injection safety). It is **fail-safe** — no declaration found emits `status:"skipped"` and absent `jq` emits `status:"unverified"` (both with `checks_total 0`) and the script ALWAYS exits 0 (mirroring `run-benchmark.sh` / `run-eval.sh`). **(2) `scripts/test-run-ground-truth.sh`** self-test (14 assertions: cmd pass/fail, empty-cmd fail, `--no-cmd`/`GROUND_TRUTH_NO_CMD` safety-valve skip, corpus-task resolution, qa-executor deferral with ran:false, no-declaration skipped, no-jq unverified, fail-safe exit-0, leading-dash target preservation, exact-heading match, GROUND_TRUTH_JSON shape). **Safety valve:** `--no-cmd` (or `GROUND_TRUTH_NO_CMD=1`) skips `cmd:`/bare checks (recorded `unverified`/`cmd_disabled`; `corpus-task:` still runs); Supervisor Phase 4.5 passes it on the unattended `--non-interactive`/`/autonomous` path so a machine-authored `cmd:` bullet never runs arbitrary shell with no human in the loop (interim guard until the Plan Reviewer control lands in slice 1b). **(3) Supervisor Phase 4.5 wiring** — Phase 4.5 invokes the runner **after** the Code Reviewer self-heal loop and folds the result into a new additive **`ground_truth`** SUPERVISOR_RESULT object plus flat `session_end` JSONL hard-signal fields. The signal is **advisory only**: it NEVER changes `heal_decision`, NEVER triggers a fix, and NEVER blocks the PR — exactly the contract-conformance / benchmark precedent. **(4) New `GROUND_TRUTH_JSON` schema** (`schema_version 1`; fields `ran` / `status: pass|advisory_failures|unverified|skipped` / `checks_total` / `checks_passed` / `pass_rate` "M/N" / `per_check[]` of `{kind, target, status: pass|fail|unverified, reason?}` / `commit` / `date`) and the `## Executable Acceptance` brief convention documented in `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md`; SUPERVISOR_RESULT stays `schema_version 1` (same additive precedent as `contract_conformance` / `benchmark_result` / `branch_base` / `pr_state`). The ground-truth runner is **DISTINCT from** the eval harness (`run-eval.sh` / `EVAL_RESULT`, output-quality over a fixed corpus) and the canary benchmark (`run-benchmark.sh` / `BENCHMARK_JSON`, the hard-signal pipeline) — kept separate by name and directory. **Deferred:** M2b slice 1b (auto-dispatch the QA Executor's Playwright/app-execution muscle for web-app repos) and M2b part-2 (auto-running the full Launch Pad→Supervisor agent loop in CI against the corpus). **No new agent / command / hook / skill (still 14 / 16 / 51 / 19); the new scripts are uncounted by the doc-currency gate; additive schema (SUPERVISOR_RESULT stays `schema_version` 1).** Additive on top of v14.18.0.

**v14.18.0 — `/insights` becomes the unified System-Twin scoreboard (deterministic run fitness + growth):** Promotes the local `/insights` dashboard into the single deterministic run-scoreboard for the System Twin's two compounding signals — its **fitness function** (eval pass-rate over time) and its **growth** (System Contract coverage over time) — **advisory, additive, and with zero counted artifacts.** **(1) `scripts/run-eval.sh` persists EVAL_RESULT history (ST1).** The eval runner now best-effort appends each run's result as exactly one JSON line to the gitignored, git-root-relative `.supervisor/eval/results.jsonl`. The recorded line is the full `EVAL_RESULT` field set (`schema_version` / `tasks_total` / `tasks_passed` / `pass_rate` / `per_task[]` / `commit` / `date` / `status`) **plus a `recorded_at` UTC timestamp** so the history is orderable independent of the contextual `date`. The append is **atomic and fail-safe** — an unwritable path, missing dir, or absent `jq` skips the record silently and the runner **still always exits 0** (the determinism invariant on the printed tallies/`per_task` is unchanged). An **`EVAL_RESULTS_FILE` env override** retargets the history file and a **`--no-record` flag** disables persistence entirely (used by the self-test). `scripts/test-run-eval.sh` extended to **9 assertions** (record append shape, `recorded_at` presence, `--no-record`, override path, fail-safe exit-0). **(2) `scripts/build-insights.sh` gains two always-rendered dashboard sections (ST2).** **Eval fitness function** renders the latest `pass_rate` plus a ` → ` trend read from `.supervisor/eval/results.jsonl`; **System Twin growth** renders the current contract count from `.supervisor/twin/contracts/*.md` and the cumulative `add`-event growth from `.supervisor/twin/.provenance.jsonl`. Both sections are **sparse-tolerant** (emit a benign "no data yet" line when their source is missing/empty), **jq-deterministic**, and **exit 0**. `commands/insights.md` gains the **distinct-from-Claude-Code-Insights / `ccusage`** "deterministic run scoreboard" framing and docs for the two new sections. `scripts/test-insights.sh` extended to **39 assertions**. **(3) Docs, version, currency (ST3).** Version bumped **14.17.0 → 14.18.0** across `plugin.json` / `marketplace.json` (description headline only — anti-rebloat: no new sentence appended, the two descriptions stay byte-identical and the counts unchanged), CLAUDE.md banner trimmed to the two most recent releases (v14.18.0 + v14.17.0; the v14.16.0 banner now lives only here), and both CI gates (`validate-version.sh`, `check-doc-currency.sh`) green. **No new counted artifacts (still 14 / 16 / 51 / 19); the new/extended scripts are uncounted by the doc-currency gate; additive schema (EVAL_RESULT stays `schema_version` 1).** Additive on top of v14.17.0.

**v14.17.0 — System Twin M2a: a deterministic eval harness (the real fitness function):** Lands the **eval instrument** slice of milestone M2 (Pillar 2, "provable-done") — **advisory, additive, and with zero counted artifacts.** **(1) `scripts/run-eval.sh`** — a deterministic runner/scorer that measures plugin *output quality* against a fixed task corpus. Each task is a self-contained `eval-corpus/<task-id>/` dir carrying an executable `check.sh` (exit 0 = pass, non-0 = fail) that deterministically verifies the task's outcome; the script prints a human/grep per-task block + a `Pass rate: M/N` line and exactly ONE machine-readable `EVAL_RESULT: {...}` line (jq-built for injection safety). The metric is the **`pass_rate` M/N** — the fitness signal tracked release-over-release. The runner is deterministic (same corpus + same checks → identical `tasks_total`/`tasks_passed`/`pass_rate`/`per_task` every run); the **determinism invariant covers the tallies/`per_task` only**, not the contextual `commit`/`date`. It is **fail-safe** — a missing corpus dir or absent `jq` emits `status:"unverified"` with `tasks_total 0`, `pass_rate "0/0"`, `per_task []`, and the script ALWAYS exits 0 (mirroring `run-benchmark.sh`). **(2) Corpus format + a 4-task seed corpus** under `scripts/eval-corpus/` (`doc-currency-green`, `eval-selftest-green`, `fixture-unit-test`, `version-consistent`). **(3) New `EVAL_RESULT` schema** (`schema_version 1`; fields `schema_version` / `tasks_total` / `tasks_passed` / `pass_rate` / `per_task[]` of `{id, status: pass|fail}` / `commit` / `date` / `status: ok|unverified`) documented in `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md`. **(4) `scripts/test-run-eval.sh`** self-test. The eval harness is **DISTINCT from the canary benchmark** (`run-benchmark.sh` / `BENCHMARK_JSON`, which validates the `session_end` hard-signal pipeline) — "eval" ≠ "benchmark", kept separate by name and directory. **M2b is explicitly deferred:** (a) auto-running the full Launch Pad→Supervisor agent loop in CI against the corpus, and (b) wiring ground-truth execution into Supervisor Phase 4.5. **No new agent / command / hook / skill (still 14 / 16 / 51 / 19); the new scripts are uncounted by the doc-currency gate; additive schema.** Additive on top of v14.16.0.

**v14.16.0 — Standalone PR review-and-heal (`/review-pr`):** A new user-facing capability that runs the bounded review→fix→re-review loop against an *existing* PR URL — decoupled from a full Supervisor run, so any open PR (human-authored or agent-authored) can be reviewed and auto-healed in place. **(1) New `review-pr` agent** (`loomwright:review-pr-runner`, user-facing) — resolves the PR-URL → head branch (`gh pr view --json headRefName`), checks it out, and orchestrates a `code-reviewer` review + a `general-purpose` fix worker over the bounded loop (default 3 iterations) until the PR diff is clean (`PASS`) or the loop escalates (`ESCALATED`); fixes are pushed (never `--force`); **never auto-merges** — the PR is always left open for a human, and `NEEDS_HUMAN` stops + notifies (`notify-desktop.sh` / `send-webhook.sh`, best-effort) + posts findings (`gh pr comment`). **(2) New `/review-pr <pr-url>` command** — the inline main-thread entry point; the `-runner` agent is for `claude --agent` agent-owned sessions and is NEVER Task-spawned (subagents-cannot-spawn-subagents, AC9). **(3) New `review-heal` skill** — the single source of truth for the loop contract, PR-URL→branch resolution, bounded-loop semantics, notification behavior, the no-auto-merge rule, and the `REVIEW_HEAL_RESULT` block (schema_version 1: `decision` `PASS|ESCALATED`, `iterations`, `issues_fixed`, `remaining_issues`, `pr_url`, `notified`). **(4) `scripts/dispatch-pr-review.sh`** — a config-file-gated (`auto_review: true` in `.supervisor/notify-config.json`), per-PR-marker-guarded, fail-safe (always exits 0) dispatcher that launches a fresh `claude --agent review-pr-runner` process (self-tested by `scripts/test-dispatch-pr-review.sh`; both **uncounted** by the doc-currency gate). **(5) Supervisor completion-tail opt-in dispatch** (off by default; `--auto-review` / `--no-auto-review` flags) and **(6) `/autonomous` EVALUATE chaining** (a `Task` step between PR-base verification and the Signal-1 gate, recording `REVIEW_HEAL_RESULT` into `iterations[]`) can hand a freshly-created PR to the loop. **`REVIEW_HEAL_RESULT` schema** documented in `docs/RESULT_SCHEMAS.md` + capability-matrix row in `docs/ARCHITECTURE_CONTRACTS.md`. Phase 4.5 is NOT rewritten — it gains only a one-line pointer to the shared skill. **Counts move 13 → 14 agents / 15 → 16 commands / 50 → 51 skills (hooks stay 19).** Additive on top of v14.15.0.

**v14.15.0 — System Twin M1 enrichment: incident history + richer blast-radius graph (advisory, propose-only):** Extends the v14.10.0 System Twin foundation along its M1 slice — **all additive, propose-only, and strictly subordinate to `CLAUDE.md`; nothing here gates a PR.** **(1) `incident_history[]` on `SYSTEM_CONTRACT`** — a new additive field recording recent per-subsystem incidents (each entry `kind ∈ conformance_violation | self_heal_fix | other`); **`schema_version` stays 1** and a missing field stays valid (same additive precedent as prior optional Twin fields, documented in `RESULT_SCHEMAS.md`). **(2) `scripts/twin-graph.sh`** — a new blast-radius helper that, given a subsystem, reports both its declared **depends-on** edges and the **derived depended-on-by** (reverse) edges across the contract store; it reads only through `read-system-contract.sh` so provenance/hash-chain verification is preserved, is **fail-safe** (no contracts / sparse store → empty graph, exit 0, never errors), and is **self-tested by `scripts/test-twin-graph.sh`** — **both scripts are uncounted** by the doc-currency gate, same precedent as the other Twin helpers. **(3) Builder records this run's incidents** — Supervisor Phase 4.5's ephemeral contract builder now appends this-run `incident_history` (bounded **≤5**, deduped, **conformance-violation / self-heal-fix only**, written **PASS-only** via the repo-root sole writer `write-system-contract.sh`). **(4) Launch Pad Phase 3** now surfaces the **full dependency graph + incident history** in its advisory blast-radius prediction, degrading gracefully when no contract exists. **No new agent / command / hook / skill (still 13 / 15 / 50 / 19); 2 new uncounted scripts; additive schema (`schema_version` stays 1).** Additive on top of v14.14.0.

**v14.14.0 — Robustness polish: doc-currency gate blind spot + two `format-twin-delta.sh` edge cases (NON-ENGINE, advisory/tooling-only):** Three small, independently-verified fixes batched into one PR (anti-rebloat — no three-tiny-PR fan-out); no agent prompt / hook / engine file is touched. **(1) Doc-currency gate blind spot** — `scripts/check-doc-currency.sh` scanned `Slash commands (N)` / `N slash commands` but **not** the `N entry points` phrasing used at `CLAUDE.md:30`, so that command-count surface could silently drift while CI stayed green; added a `[0-9]+ entry points` command-count pattern, scoped to the count phrasing so prose like "entry points, trust boundaries" never false-positives (verified: gate still passes on the current tree, and a deliberately-stale count is now caught). **(2) `format-twin-delta.sh` note A** — documented near `is_int` that the guard accepts **integer-valued metrics only** (today's only benchmark producer emits integer counts) and to widen it to a single decimal point if a float-valued metric is ever introduced (the downstream sign logic is already string-based and float-safe); **no behavior change**. **(3) `format-twin-delta.sh` note B** — the conformance segment now emits `(N violations)` **only when `--violations` is a valid integer** (including genuine `0`); a null/non-numeric count **omits** the parenthetical instead of fabricating `(0 violations)`, so `advisory_violations` + unparseable renders `Twin: conformance ADVISORY_VIOLATIONS` (no self-contradictory count) while `pass` + `0` stays `Twin: conformance PASS (0 violations)`. `test-format-twin-delta.sh` updated to assert the omitted-count form for both the omitted and non-numeric cases, plus a genuine-`0` and an `advisory_violations`+unparseable case (self-test green at 32 assertions ≥ prior 29). The Twin delta line stays **purely advisory** — it NEVER gates the PR. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (still 13 / 15 / 50 / 19)** and **no schema change.** Additive on top of v14.13.0.

**v14.13.0 — Clickable desktop notifications (opt-in, macOS):** Makes the plugin's pause-event desktop banner **clickable** when [`terminal-notifier`](https://github.com/julienXX/terminal-notifier) is installed — a click brings the **Claude desktop app to the foreground** (default `activate` mode; for a single active session that lands the user back on it). A new **self-tested pure helper** `scripts/notify-click-target.sh` (tested by `scripts/test-notify-click-target.sh` — 15 cases incl. injection-safety; neither counted by the doc-currency gate) decides the click action and constructs an opt-in `claude://claude.ai/resume?session=<uuid>` deep link (reverse-engineered from `Claude.app`'s deep-link router: host `gh.ClaudeAI="claude.ai"`, path `lg.Resume="resume"`, UUID-validated `session` param → `importCliSession`). `notify-desktop.sh` prefers `terminal-notifier` — all calls **detached** so the hook never blocks the agent loop (this host has neither `timeout` nor `gtimeout`, so `run_bounded` can't bound a lingering notifier), `-group`-coalesced to bound orphan pileup, and deliberately **omitting `-sender`** — and falls back to the existing `osascript` banner when `terminal-notifier` is absent, so behavior is **byte-identical (zero regression)** for users who don't install it. **New env var** `LOOMWRIGHT_NOTIFY_CLICK` (`activate` (default) | `resume` (opt-in deep link) | `auto` | `off`). **macOS 26 findings baked into the defaults (verified live):** (a) impersonating Claude's bundle id via `-sender` to borrow its icon is **silently dropped** by the macOS 26 notification service — the banner never appears — so `-sender` is unused (the banner shows `terminal-notifier`'s own icon); (b) `terminal-notifier`'s `-open`/`-execute` click callback **no longer fires** on macOS 26 (it relied on the deprecated `NSUserNotification` delegate), so a clicked `resume` banner can't navigate there — only a direct `open` of the URL does — which is why `activate` (handled OS-natively) is the reliable default. True per-session navigation from a notification *click* on macOS 26 would require a custom signed helper app using `UNUserNotificationCenter` (out of scope). The leftover `osascript` fallback's known quirk (clicking it opens *Script Editor*) only affects users without `terminal-notifier`. **No new agent / command / skill / hook (still 13 / 15 / 50 / 19)** and **no schema change.** Additive on top of v14.12.0.

**v14.12.0 — Twin inline-delta: surface *this run's* hard signal in the SELF_HEAL completion tail (Level 1):** Adds one **advisory, human-readable line** to the Supervisor Phase 4.5 SELF_HEAL completion tail summarizing *this run's* System Twin hard signal — contract-conformance (status + violation count) and benchmark (status + value + Δ) — built from the values **already computed** earlier in Phase 4.5 (`SUPERVISOR_RESULT.contract_conformance` / `.benchmark_result` and the matching flat `session_end` fields). Example: `Twin: conformance PASS (0 violations) · benchmark system-twin-selftest 6 (Δ +1)`. The line is **purely informational**: it NEVER changes `heal_decision`, NEVER blocks or alters the PR, and NEVER touches control flow — gate behavior is byte-identical with or without it. The formatting lives in a **self-tested script** (`scripts/format-twin-delta.sh`, self-tested by `scripts/test-format-twin-delta.sh` — mirroring the `test-system-contract.sh` pattern; neither counted by the doc-currency gate); the `agents/supervisor.md` change is a minimal **call + echo** in the completion tail with no new control flow. **Graceful / sparse:** a run with no Twin signal (Twin not exercised, contract absent, or fields null) prints a benign `Twin: no signal this run` (or nothing) and the script ALWAYS exits 0. **Level 2** (trend/threshold nudges like "conformance ↓ N runs → run /dreaming") is explicitly **OUT OF SCOPE**, deferred until real run-history exists to calibrate thresholds.
